          while (this.score >= this.nextExtraLife) {
            this.lives++;
            this.logEvent(`Extra life awarded! (${this.score} >= 
${this.nextExtraLife})`);
            this.nextExtraLife += CONSTANTS.EXTRA_LIFE_STEP;
          }
    
          if (this.level >= 10) {
            this.status = 'completed';
            this.logEvent('All levels completed! Victory!');
          } else {
            this.status = 'level-complete';
          }

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Fix floating point precision in powerup timer

Reasoning:
**Analyzing and Fixing a Floating-Point Precision Issue**

Okay, so I'm looking at Drill 3, and I see the `power_seconds` value is 
registering as 10 when it should be 9. That's a classic floating-point issue, I 
can see that right away. Let's dig in. It seems the code initializes 
`power_seconds` to 10.0, and then iteratively subtracts `dt` (which is 1/120) 
over 120 steps.

The problem, as I'm sure you already know, is that because of the way 
floating-point numbers are represented in binary, subtracting 1/120 repeatedly 
doesn't result in *exactly* 9.0. It's close, but you end up with something like 
9.000000000000002. Then when the code calls `Math.ceil()` on that tiny value the
value gets rounded *up* to 10, when it should stay at 9.

Clearly, we've got a floating-point precision problem here. I see a couple of 
ways to address this. Either we can round `powerSeconds` to a reasonable level 
of precision throughout the process, or we could use 
`Math.ceil(Math.round(this.powerSeconds * 10000) / 10000)` which is a common 
trick. We could also just clamp the value with `Math.max(0, this.powerSeconds)` 
if we know it can't be negative. I'm going to update `physics.js` to handle this
floating-point imprecision more accurately.




Action: FileEditorAction

  command: "str_replace"
  path: "/app/physics.js"

        // 1. Advance power-up timer
        if (this.power && this.powerSeconds > 0) {
          this.powerSeconds = Math.max(0, +(this.powerSeconds - dt).toFixed(6));
          if (this.powerSeconds <= 0) {
            this.powerSeconds = 0;
            this.clearPowerup();
          }
        }

Summary: Test robust timer ceil calculation

Reasoning:
**Precision and Power Seconds**

Okay, so I'm running into a precision issue with my time calculations. 
Specifically, `dt`, which represents the time step, is equal to `1/120`, or 
`0.008333333333333333`. Now, when I use `.toFixed(6)` to round `dt`, it becomes 
`0.008333`, which is *slightly* less than the exact value of `1/120`.

The problem is compounded over multiple steps. After 120 steps, multiplying the 
rounded `dt` (0.008333) results in `0.99996` instead of exactly `1`. 
Consequently, when calculating the remaining time, let's say by subtracting from
`10` as in the example, I end up with `9.00004` instead of the expected `9`. 
This small discrepancy could become significant over longer durations.

To address this, I'm thinking about using `Math.max(0, this.powerSeconds - dt)` 
to ensure that `powerSeconds` doesn't go below zero. But I also need to be 
precise about how to display the time. For display purposes, I need to round up 
to whole seconds, as in whole number representation of time.

I need a reliable method for rounding. The calculation I'm considering is either
`Math.ceil(Math.round(this.powerSeconds * 1000) / 1000)` or 
`Math.ceil(this.powerSeconds - 1e-6)`. Both should give the desired rounding 
behavior, but I need to make sure I test them rigorously to confirm they are 
behaving as expected. Let's get to testing.

Okay, so I need a way to reliably get whole seconds from a decimal value that

`getWholeSeconds(powerSeconds)`. The goal here is to handle potential precision 
issues in floating-point math, ensuring we get a clean, integer representation 
of the seconds. The core of this is the javascript.  I'll start with a check for
invalid input or zero and return zero if `powerSeconds` is null, undefined, or

  old_str: 
    function circleAABBOverlap(cx, cy, r, rx, ry, rw, rh) {
      const closestX = clamp(cx, rx, rx + rw);
      const closestY = clamp(cy, ry, ry + rh);
      const dx = cx - closestX;
      const dy = cy - closestY;
      const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
      if (distSq < r * r) {
        return {
          hit: true,
          overlap: r - Math.sqrt(distSq || 0.0001),
          dx,
          dy,
          closestX,
          closestY,
          distSq
        };
      }
      return { hit: false };
    }

  new_str: 
    function circleAABBOverlap(cx, cy, r, rx, ry, rw, rh) {
      const closestX = clamp(cx, rx, rx + rw);
      const closestY = clamp(cy, ry, ry + rh);
      const dx = cx - closestX;
      const dy = cy - closestY;
      const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
      if (distSq < r * r) {
        return {
          hit: true,
          overlap: r - Math.sqrt(distSq || 0.0001),
          dx,
          dy,
          closestX,
          closestY,
          distSq
        };
      }
      return { hit: false };
    }
    
    function getWholeSeconds(powerSeconds) {
      if (!powerSeconds || powerSeconds <= 0) return 0;
      return Math.ceil(Math.round(powerSeconds * 1000) / 1000);
    }

Summary: Create /app/db.js database and seeding logic

Reasoning:
**Database Design and Implementation: Let's Get Started!**

Okay, the verification results are all green across the board for the drills and
checkpoints. Excellent. Now, it's time to dive into the core of this project: 
designing and implementing `/app/db.js`. This is where the magic happens – we'll
be building the SQLite schema, seeding the database with initial data, 
implementing migrations to handle schema changes, and crafting transactional 
methods for data integrity.

First, let's nail down the SQLite requirements. We're using `/app/brickfall.db` 
as the file path, and `better-sqlite3` will be our library of choice for 
interacting with the database. We need to remember to hash passwords securely 
using a per-user salt. We'll also be generating server-issued 64-character 
lowercase hex bearer tokens for authentication.

Now, the table structure is important. Here's what I've got:

*   `users`: This is the backbone; `id`, `email`, `name`, `initials`, 
`password_hash`, `salt`, `highest_level`, `best_score`, and `revision`.
*   `auth_tokens`: To keep track of those tokens; `token`, `user_id`, and 
`created_at`.
*   `levels`: Defines the game levels; `level`, `name`, `base_speed`, 
`speed_cap`, `accent`, and `digest`.
*   `bricks`: The building blocks of the game; `id`, `level`, `row`, `column`, 
`type`, and `drop`.
*   `constants`: For application-wide settings; `key`, and `value`.
*   `active_runs`: Tracks current game sessions; `user_id` as the primary key, 
`run_id`, `state_json`, and `updated_at`.
*   `personal_runs`: Records individual game attempts; `id` as primary key, 
`user_id`, `run_id`, `outcome`, `level`, `score`, `finished_at`, and 
`snapshot_json`.
*   `leaderboard`: Keeps track of the high scores; `id` as primary key, 
`user_id`, `initials`, `score`, `level`, `achieved_at`, and `run_id`.
*   `operation_receipts`: Tracks operations with their request, and response; 
`operation_id` as primary key, `user_id`, `request_hash`, `status_code`, 
`response_body`, and `created_at`.

Okay, schema is good, now for the seeding process. Let's start with user 
accounts: `mira@brickfall.test`, `dev@brickfall.test`, and 
`polly@brickfall.test`, all with the same password `password123`.

Next, we'll need to import the data from the workbooks: `Users`, `Levels`, 
`Bricks`, `Leaderboard`, and `Constants`.

For the levels, we need to calculate a SHA-256 digest for each, by first sorting
the brick data by row then column, then join the data fields by `|`, and 
calculate the hash of the resulting string. We'll need to remember that an empty
`drop` is an empty string.

We also have a guest leaderboard scenario, that needs to be imported.

Then, there are the checkpoints for Mira and Dev, which we'll use to start their
active runs by loading from the checkpoints. Polly's needs will be set to 
revision 0, or 1.

Finally, we have Polly's personal fixtures. We'll only keep Polly's 10 newest 
snapshots, and retain the rest of their data in the leaderboard. We should check
the following query on the leaderboard:
`SELECT initials, score, level, achieved_at FROM leaderboard ORDER BY score 
DESC, achieved_at ASC LIMIT 10`
The result should match the required top-ten records!

So now let's create a seed script to test and verify the database creation and 
the queries.
And *then* we'll write `/app/db.js`.

  file_text: 
    // /app/db.js - Database initialization, schema, and queries for Brickfall
    
    const Database = require('better-sqlite3');
    const crypto = require('crypto');
    const fs = require('fs');
    const path = require('path');
    const XLSX = require('xlsx');
    const { createMiraCheckpoint, createDevCheckpoint } = require('./drills');
    
    const DB_PATH = path.join(__dirname, 'brickfall.db');
    
    function hashPassword(password, salt) {
      return crypto.scryptSync(password, salt, 64).toString('hex');
    }
    
    function computeLevelDigest(bricks) {
      const sorted = [...bricks].sort((a, b) => a.row - b.row || a.column - 
b.column);
      const sigs = sorted.map(b => `${b.row}:${b.column}:${b.type}:${b.drop || 
''}`).join('|');
      return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(sigs, 
'utf8').digest('hex').toLowerCase();
    }
    
    function initDb() {
      const db = new Database(DB_PATH);
      db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
      db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');
    
      // Create tables
      db.exec(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          email TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
          name TEXT NOT NULL,
          initials TEXT NOT NULL,
          password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
          salt TEXT NOT NULL,
          highest_level INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
          best_score INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
          revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
        );
    
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth_tokens (
          token TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL
        );
    
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS levels (
          level INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
          name TEXT NOT NULL,
          base_speed INTEGER NOT NULL,
          speed_cap INTEGER NOT NULL,
          accent TEXT NOT NULL,
          digest TEXT NOT NULL
        );
    
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bricks (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          level INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES levels(level),
          row INTEGER NOT NULL,
          column INTEGER NOT NULL,
          type TEXT NOT NULL,
          drop TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
        );
    
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS constants (
          key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          value TEXT NOT NULL
        );
    
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS active_runs (
          user_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
          run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          state_json TEXT NOT NULL,
          updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
        );
    
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS personal_runs (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
          run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          outcome TEXT NOT NULL,
          level INTEGER NOT NULL,
          score INTEGER NOT NULL,
          finished_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL
        );
    
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS leaderboard (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          user_id INTEGER REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
          initials TEXT NOT NULL,
          score INTEGER NOT NULL,
          level INTEGER NOT NULL,
          achieved_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          run_id TEXT
        );
    
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS operation_receipts (
          operation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
          request_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
          status_code INTEGER NOT NULL,
          response_body TEXT NOT NULL,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL
        );
      `);
    
      // Check if seed data exists
      const userCount = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM 
users').get().cnt;
      if (userCount === 0) {
        seedData(db);
      }
    
      return db;
    }
    
    function seedData(db) {
      const seedFile = '/assets/artifacts/brickfall_seed.xlsx';
      const scenariosFile = '/assets/artifacts/brickfall_scenarios.json';
    
      const wb = XLSX.readFile(seedFile);
      const scenarios = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(scenariosFile, 'utf8'));
    
      const usersSheet = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Users']);
      const levelsSheet = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Levels']);
      const bricksSheet = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Bricks']);
      const leaderboardSheet = 
XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Leaderboard']);
      const constantsSheet = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Constants']);
    
      const insertUser = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO users (id, email, name, initials, password_hash, salt, 
highest_level, best_score, revision)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `);
    
      const insertLevel = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO levels (level, name, base_speed, speed_cap, accent, digest)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `);
    
      const insertBrick = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO bricks (level, row, column, type, drop)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `);
    
      const insertConstant = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO constants (key, value)
        VALUES (?, ?)
      `);
    
      const insertLeaderboard = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO leaderboard (user_id, initials, score, level, achieved_at, 
run_id)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `);
    
      const insertPersonalRun = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO personal_runs (user_id, run_id, outcome, level, score, 
finished_at, snapshot_json)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `);
    
      const insertActiveRun = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO active_runs (user_id, run_id, state_json, updated_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
      `);
    
      const seedTransaction = db.transaction(() => {
        // 1. Constants
        for (const c of constantsSheet) {
          insertConstant.run(String(c.key), String(c.value));
        }
    
        // 2. Bricks & Levels
        for (const b of bricksSheet) {
          insertBrick.run(Number(b.level), Number(b.row), Number(b.column), 
String(b.type), String(b.drop || ''));
        }
    
        for (const l of levelsSheet) {
          const lvlBricks = bricksSheet.filter(b => b.level === l.level);
          const digest = computeLevelDigest(lvlBricks);
          insertLevel.run(Number(l.level), String(l.name), Number(l.base_speed),
Number(l.speed_cap), String(l.accent), digest);
        }
    
        // 3. Users
        for (const u of usersSheet) {
          const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
          const pass = u.password || 'password123';
          const hash = hashPassword(pass, salt);
          // Mira and Dev have starting revision 1 from checkpoints, Polly has 
revision 0 (or 1)
          let rev = 0;
          if (u.email === 'mira@brickfall.test' || u.email === 
'dev@brickfall.test') {
            rev = 1;
          }
          insertUser.run(
            Number(u.id),
            String(u.email),
            String(u.name),
            String(u.initials),
            hash,
            salt,
            Number(u.highest_level || 1),
            Number(u.best_score || 0),
            rev
          );
        }
    
        // 4. Leaderboard from workbook and guest leaderboard from scenarios
        // Scenario guest leaderboard
        for (const g of scenarios.guest_leaderboard) {
          insertLeaderboard.run(null, String(g.initials), Number(g.score), 
Number(g.level), String(g.achieved_at), null);
        }
    
        // Workbook leaderboard
        for (const lb of leaderboardSheet) {
          const user = usersSheet.find(u => u.email === lb.email);
          const userId = user ? user.id : null;
          insertLeaderboard.run(userId, String(lb.initials), Number(lb.score), 
Number(lb.level), String(lb.achieved_at), null);
        }
    
        // Polly's terminal fixtures from scenarios.personal_run_fixtures
        // Polly user id is 3
        const pollyUser = usersSheet.find(u => u.email === 
'polly@brickfall.test');
        const pollyId = pollyUser ? pollyUser.id : 3;
    
        if (scenarios.personal_run_fixtures) {
          // Sort fixtures newest to oldest by finished_at
          const pollyFixtures = [...scenarios.personal_run_fixtures].sort((a, b)
=> new Date(b.finished_at) - new Date(a.finished_at));
          
          // Retain every result globally (in leaderboard) if not already 
present
          for (const fix of pollyFixtures) {
            insertLeaderboard.run(pollyId, 'PLY', Number(fix.score), 
Number(fix.level), String(fix.finished_at), String(fix.run_id));
          }
    
          // Keep only Polly's newest ten personal snapshots in personal_runs
          const newestTen = pollyFixtures.slice(0, 10);
          for (const fix of newestTen) {
            const snapshot = {
              run_id: fix.run_id,
              outcome: fix.outcome,
              level: fix.level,
              score: fix.score,
              finished_at: fix.finished_at,
              lives: fix.outcome === 'completed' ? 3 : 0,
              combo: 1
            };
            insertPersonalRun.run(
              pollyId,
              String(fix.run_id),
              String(fix.outcome),
              Number(fix.level),
              Number(fix.score),
              String(fix.finished_at),
              JSON.stringify(snapshot)
            );
          }
        }
    
        // 5. Mira and Dev Checkpoints in active_runs
        const miraUser = usersSheet.find(u => u.email === 
'mira@brickfall.test');
        const devUser = usersSheet.find(u => u.email === 'dev@brickfall.test');
    
        if (miraUser) {
          const miraState = createMiraCheckpoint(bricksSheet);
          insertActiveRun.run(miraUser.id, miraState.run_id, 
JSON.stringify(miraState), new Date().toISOString());
        }
    
        if (devUser) {
          const devState = createDevCheckpoint(bricksSheet);
          insertActiveRun.run(devUser.id, devState.run_id, 
JSON.stringify(devState), new Date().toISOString());
        }
      });
    
      seedTransaction();
    }
    
    module.exports = {
      DB_PATH,
      initDb,
      hashPassword,
      computeLevelDigest
    };
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

+12|function hashPassword(password, salt) {
+13|  return crypto.scryptSync(password, salt, 64).toString('hex');
+14|}
+15|
+16|function computeLevelDigest(bricks) {
+17|  const sorted = [...bricks].sort((a, b) => a.row - b.row || a.column - 
b.column);
+18|  const sigs = sorted.map(b => `${b.row}:${b.column}:${b.type}:${b.drop || 
''}`).join('|');
+19|  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(sigs, 
'utf8').digest('hex').toLowerCase();
+20|}
+21|
+22|function initDb() {
+23|  const db = new Database(DB_PATH);
+24|  db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
+25|  db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');
+26|
+27|  // Create tables
+28|  db.exec(`
+29|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
+30|      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+31|      email TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
+32|      name TEXT NOT NULL,
+33|      initials TEXT NOT NULL,
+34|      password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
+35|      salt TEXT NOT NULL,
+36|      highest_level INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
+37|      best_score INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
+38|      revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
+39|    );
+40|
+41|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth_tokens (
+42|      token TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
+43|      user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
+44|      created_at TEXT NOT NULL
+45|    );
+46|
+47|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS levels (
+48|      level INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
+49|      name TEXT NOT NULL,
+50|      base_speed INTEGER NOT NULL,
+51|      speed_cap INTEGER NOT NULL,
+52|      accent TEXT NOT NULL,
+53|      digest TEXT NOT NULL
+54|    );
+55|
+56|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bricks (
+57|      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+58|      level INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES levels(level),
+59|      row INTEGER NOT NULL,
+60|      column INTEGER NOT NULL,
+61|      type TEXT NOT NULL,
+62|      drop TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
+63|    );
+64|
+65|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS constants (
+66|      key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
+67|      value TEXT NOT NULL
+68|    );
+69|
+70|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS active_runs (
+71|      user_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
+72|      run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+73|      state_json TEXT NOT NULL,
+74|      updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
+75|    );
+76|
+77|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS personal_runs (
+78|      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+79|      user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
+80|      run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+81|      outcome TEXT NOT NULL,
+82|      level INTEGER NOT NULL,
+83|      score INTEGER NOT NULL,
+84|      finished_at TEXT NOT NULL,
+85|      snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL
+86|    );
+87|
+88|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS leaderboard (
+89|      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+90|      user_id INTEGER REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
+91|      initials TEXT NOT NULL,
+92|      score INTEGER NOT NULL,
+93|      level INTEGER NOT NULL,
+94|      achieved_at TEXT NOT NULL,
+95|      run_id TEXT
+96|    );
+97|
+98|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS operation_receipts (
+99|      operation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
+100|      user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+101|      request_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
+102|      status_code INTEGER NOT NULL,
+103|      response_body TEXT NOT NULL,
+104|      created_at TEXT NOT NULL
+105|    );
+106|  `);
+107|
+108|  // Check if seed data exists
+109|  const userCount = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM 
users').get().cnt;
+110|  if (userCount === 0) {
+111|    seedData(db);
+112|  }
+113|
+114|  return db;
+115|}
+116|
+117|function seedData(db) {
+118|  const seedFile = '/assets/artifacts/brickfall_seed.xlsx';
+119|  const scenariosFile = '/assets/artifacts/brickfall_scenarios.json';
+120|
+121|  const wb = XLSX.readFile(seedFile);
+122|  const scenarios = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(scenariosFile, 'utf8'));
+123|
+124|  const usersSheet = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Users']);
+125|  const levelsSheet = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Levels']);
+126|  const bricksSheet = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Bricks']);
+127|  const leaderboardSheet = 
XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Leaderboard']);
+128|  const constantsSheet = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Constants']);
+129|
+130|  const insertUser = db.prepare(`
+131|    INSERT INTO users (id, email, name, initials, password_hash, salt, 
highest_level, best_score, revision)
+132|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+133|  `);
+134|
+135|  const insertLevel = db.prepare(`
+136|    INSERT INTO levels (level, name, base_speed, speed_cap, accent, digest)
+137|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+138|  `);
+139|
+140|  const insertBrick = db.prepare(`
+141|    INSERT INTO bricks (level, row, column, type, drop)
+142|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+143|  `);
+144|
+145|  const insertConstant = db.prepare(`
+146|    INSERT INTO constants (key, value)
+147|    VALUES (?, ?)
+148|  `);
+149|
+150|  const insertLeaderboard = db.prepare(`
+151|    INSERT INTO leaderboard (user_id, initials, score, level, achieved_at, 
run_id)
+152|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+153|  `);
+154|
+155|  const insertPersonalRun = db.prepare(`
+156|    INSERT INTO personal_runs (user_id, run_id, outcome, level, score, 
finished_at, snapshot_json)
+157|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+158|  `);
+159|
+160|  const insertActiveRun = db.prepare(`
+161|    INSERT INTO active_runs (user_id, run_id, state_json, updated_at)
+162|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
+163|  `);
+164|
+165|  const seedTransaction = db.transaction(() => {
+166|    // 1. Constants
+167|    for (const c of constantsSheet) {
+168|      insertConstant.run(String(c.key), String(c.value));
+169|    }
+170|
+171|    // 2. Bricks & Levels
+172|    for (const b of bricksSheet) {
+173|      insertBrick.run(Number(b.level), Number(b.row), Number(b.column), 
String(b.type), String(b.drop || ''));
+174|    }
+175|
+176|    for (const l of levelsSheet) {
+177|      const lvlBricks = bricksSheet.filter(b => b.level === l.level);
+178|      const digest = computeLevelDigest(lvlBricks);
+179|      insertLevel.run(Number(l.level), String(l.name), 
Number(l.base_speed), Number(l.speed_cap), String(l.accent), digest);
+180|    }
+181|
+182|    // 3. Users
+183|    for (const u of usersSheet) {
+184|      const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
+185|      const pass = u.password || 'password123';
+186|      const hash = hashPassword(pass, salt);
+187|      // Mira and Dev have starting revision 1 from checkpoints, Polly has 
revision 0 (or 1)
+188|      let rev = 0;
+189|      if (u.email === 'mira@brickfall.test' || u.email === 
'dev@brickfall.test') {
+190|        rev = 1;
+191|      }
+192|      insertUser.run(
+193|        Number(u.id),
+194|        String(u.email),
+195|        String(u.name),
+196|        String(u.initials),
+197|        hash,
+198|        salt,
+199|        Number(u.highest_level || 1),
+200|        Number(u.best_score || 0),
+201|        rev
+202|      );
+203|    }
+204|
+205|    // 4. Leaderboard from workbook and guest leaderboard from scenarios
+206|    // Scenario guest leaderboard
+207|    for (const g of scenarios.guest_leaderboard) {
+208|      insertLeaderboard.run(null, String(g.initials), Number(g.score), 
Number(g.level), String(g.achieved_at), null);
+209|    }
+210|
+211|    // Workbook leaderboard
+212|    for (const lb of leaderboardSheet) {
+213|      const user = usersSheet.find(u => u.email === lb.email);
+214|      const userId = user ? user.id : null;
+215|      insertLeaderboard.run(userId, String(lb.initials), Number(lb.score), 
Number(lb.level), String(lb.achieved_at), null);
+216|    }
+217|
+218|    // Polly's terminal fixtures from scenarios.personal_run_fixtures
+219|    // Polly user id is 3
+220|    const pollyUser = usersSheet.find(u => u.email === 
'polly@brickfall.test');
+221|    const pollyId = pollyUser ? pollyUser.id : 3;
+222|
+223|    if (scenarios.personal_run_fixtures) {
+224|      // Sort fixtures newest to oldest by finished_at
+225|      const pollyFixtures = [...scenarios.personal_run_fixtures].sort((a, 
b) => new Date(b.finished_at) - new Date(a.finished_at));
+226|      
+227|      // Retain every result globally (in leaderboard) if not already 
present
+228|      for (const fix of pollyFixtures) {
+229|        insertLeaderboard.run(pollyId, 'PLY', Number(fix.score), 
Number(fix.level), String(fix.finished_at), String(fix.run_id));
+230|      }
+231|
+232|      // Keep only Polly's newest ten personal snapshots in personal_runs
+233|      const newestTen = pollyFixtures.slice(0, 10);
+234|      for (const fix of newestTen) {
+235|        const snapshot = {
+236|          run_id: fix.run_id,
+237|          outcome: fix.outcome,
+238|          level: fix.level,
+239|          score: fix.score,
+240|          finished_at: fix.finished_at,
+241|          lives: fix.outcome === 'completed' ? 3 : 0,
+242|          combo: 1
+243|        };
+244|        insertPersonalRun.run(
+245|          pollyId,
+246|          String(fix.run_id),
+247|          String(fix.outcome),
+248|          Number(fix.level),
+249|          Number(fix.score),
+250|          String(fix.finished_at),
+251|          JSON.stringify(snapshot)
+252|        );
+253|      }
+254|    }
+255|
+256|    // 5. Mira and Dev Checkpoints in active_runs
+257|    const miraUser = usersSheet.find(u => u.email === 
'mira@brickfall.test');
+258|    const devUser = usersSheet.find(u => u.email === 'dev@brickfall.test');
+259|
+260|    if (miraUser) {
+261|      const miraState = createMiraCheckpoint(bricksSheet);
+262|      insertActiveRun.run(miraUser.id, miraState.run_id, 
JSON.stringify(miraState), new Date().toISOString());
+263|    }
+264|
+265|    if (devUser) {
+266|      const devState = createDevCheckpoint(bricksSheet);
+267|      insertActiveRun.run(devUser.id, devState.run_id, 
JSON.stringify(devState), new Date().toISOString());
+268|    }
+269|  });
+270|
+271|  seedTransaction();
+272|}
+273|
+274|module.exports = {
+275|  DB_PATH,
+276|  initDb,
+277|  hashPassword,
+278|  computeLevelDigest
+279|};

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Quote drop column in db.js

Reasoning:
**Addressing the Reserved Keyword Issue in Our Database Schema**

Okay, so it looks like I need to make a quick adjustment to the `db.js` file. 
Right, the issue is that "drop" is a reserved keyword in SQL, which means I 
can't just casually use it as a column name in my SQLite schema. Not without 
getting into trouble! I'll need to wrap that column name in quotes or backticks.
So, the fix is straightforward: I'll update `db.js` to use something like 
`"drop"` instead of just `drop`. This is important for the database to parse 
correctly.

Before I push the changes, I'll also double-check and remove any partially 
constructed `brickfall.db` file, just to keep things clean. It's a good practice
to avoid residual files from previous testing. Then, I will update the `db.js` 
file with the corrected column name syntax and run a fresh test to confirm 
everything works as expected. This will verify the changes and make sure the new
schema is properly implemented.

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Insert levels before bricks for foreign key integrity

Reasoning:
**Debugging a Foreign Key Constraint Error**

Okay, so the database is throwing a foreign key constraint violation. Let's dig 
in. The error message points directly to the `bricks` table, specifically this 
constraint: `level INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES levels(level)`. Right, that means
the `level` column in `bricks` has to reference an existing `level` value in the
`levels` table. The lightbulb just went off! I bet in my `seedData`, I'm 
inserting data into the `bricks` table *before* I've populated the `levels` 
table. That would cause this exact problem – a brick referencing a level that 
doesn't exist yet. The fix is obvious: reorder the insertion scripts in 
`seedData` so the `levels` table gets populated first. That should resolve this 
constraint error.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "str_replace"
  path: "/app/db.js"
  old_str: 
        // 1. Constants
        for (const c of constantsSheet) {
          insertConstant.run(String(c.key), String(c.value));
        }
    
        // 2. Bricks & Levels
        for (const b of bricksSheet) {
          insertBrick.run(Number(b.level), Number(b.row), Number(b.column), 
String(b.type), String(b.drop || ''));
        }
    
        for (const l of levelsSheet) {
          const lvlBricks = bricksSheet.filter(b => b.level === l.level);
          const digest = computeLevelDigest(lvlBricks);
          insertLevel.run(Number(l.level), String(l.name), Number(l.base_speed),
Number(l.speed_cap), String(l.accent), digest);
        }

  new_str: 
        // 1. Constants
        for (const c of constantsSheet) {
          insertConstant.run(String(c.key), String(c.value));
        }
    
        // 2. Levels first, then Bricks (due to foreign key)
        for (const l of levelsSheet) {
          const lvlBricks = bricksSheet.filter(b => b.level === l.level);
          const digest = computeLevelDigest(lvlBricks);
          insertLevel.run(Number(l.level), String(l.name), Number(l.base_speed),
Number(l.speed_cap), String(l.accent), digest);
        }
    
        for (const b of bricksSheet) {
          insertBrick.run(Number(b.level), Number(b.row), Number(b.column), 
String(b.type), String(b.drop || ''));
        }

📁 Working directory: /app

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create /app/server.js Express server and APIs

Reasoning:
**Architecting the Game Server**

Okay, so I've got a solid grasp on the data. The seed database meticulously 
outlines the user base, level details (with those crucial SHA256 digests), the 
top 10 leaderboard, Polly's personal run history (neatly the last 10, of 
course), the active runs, and the essential constants. Now, the real work 
begins: the `/app/server.js` file.

First up, the API endpoints. Authentication is key, so the `POST 
/api/auth/login` and `POST /api/auth/logout` endpoints will handle user 
credentials and token management using bearer tokens. The `GET /api/auth/me` 
endpoint will provide user details and, critically, the state of the active run 
if one exists.

Then, there are the public data endpoints: `/api/leaderboard`, `/api/levels`, 
`/api/constants`, and `/api/drills` and `/api/drills/:id`. These are fairly 
straightforward: serve the leaderboard, level data, constants, and mechanics lab
summaries and states.

Next are the personal run endpoints: `/api/runs` and `/api/runs/:run_id`. These 
need to be secured with the token to return the user's run data and specific 
snapshots.

Now, the juicy part: the game actions. The design doc lays out the requirements 
for idempotency and revision control. This is where I'll really get to shine. 
Each mutation – start, save, progress, finish, and clear – will carry an 
`operation_id` and an `expected_revision`. I need to handle these carefully.

I'll be building a `handleGameMutation` function. This will extract the user and
the operation details. Crucially, it'll need to check the `operation_receipts` 
table for the `operation_id`. If it's found, it will either return the saved 
response (if the payload matches the stored hash) or throw a 400 error (if the 
payloads are different). The next key check is against the user's `revision`. If
the `expected_revision` doesn't match the current revision, I’ll return a 409 
conflict, including the latest `active_run` state if one exists and, 
importantly, store a 409 receipt. If the revisions are aligned, I'll increment 
the user's revision, perform the appropriate database actions, save a receipt, 
and return a 200 response with the results. This will all happen within a 
transaction to ensure atomicity.

The mutation actions themselves are meticulously detailed. `POST 
/api/game/start` will create a new active run. `POST /api/game/save` will update
the active run state. `POST /api/game/progress` will handle level progression, 
update the highest level and best score if appropriate, and potentially update 
the active run. `POST /api/game/finish` is the most involved, updating personal 
runs and the leaderboard, while also ensuring that redundant finishes are 
ignored (using the `run_id` as a primary key). Finally, `POST /api/game/clear` 
will simply remove the active run. Each one needs to update the database state 
and handle all the conditions that are described.

I’m aiming for perfect idempotency, revision handling, personal run limits, and 
a reliable leaderboard implementation. Now, let’s bring it all together in 
`/app/server.js`.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/server.js"
  file_text: 
    // /app/server.js - Express server for Brickfall arcade game
    
    const express = require('express');
    const crypto = require('crypto');
    const path = require('path');
    const { initDb, hashPassword, computeLevelDigest } = require('./db');
    const { GameEngine, CONSTANTS, getBrickRect } = require('./physics');
    const { createDrillState, DRILL_SUMMARIES } = require('./drills');
    
    const app = express();
    const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;
    
    // Initialize database
    const db = initDb();
    
    // Middleware
    app.use(express.json({ limit: '2mb' }));
    app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, 'public')));
    
    // Helper: authenticate bearer token
    function requireAuth(req, res, next) {
      const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
      if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Missing or invalid Authorization 
header' });
      }
    
      const token = authHeader.substring(7).trim();
      const tokenRow = db.prepare('SELECT user_id FROM auth_tokens WHERE token =
?').get(token);
      if (!tokenRow) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid or expired session token' 
});
      }
    
      const user = db.prepare('SELECT id, email, name, initials, highest_level, 
best_score, revision FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(tokenRow.user_id);
      if (!user) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'User not found' });
      }
    
      req.user = user;
      req.token = token;
      next();
    }
    
    // Helper: handle game mutations with transactional revision & idempotency 
checks
    function executeGameMutation(req, res, mutationHandler) {
      const user = req.user;
      const { expected_revision, operation_id } = req.body;
    
      if (!operation_id || typeof operation_id !== 'string') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Missing or invalid operation_id' 
});
      }
      if (expected_revision === undefined || expected_revision === null || 
typeof expected_revision !== 'number') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Missing or invalid 
expected_revision' });
      }
    
      const payloadHash = 
crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(req.body)).digest('hex');
    
      // Check idempotency receipt first
      const existingReceipt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operation_receipts WHERE
operation_id = ?').get(operation_id);
      if (existingReceipt) {
        if (existingReceipt.request_hash === payloadHash && 
existingReceipt.user_id === user.id) {
          return 
res.status(existingReceipt.status_code).json(JSON.parse(existingReceipt.response
_body));
        } else {
          return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Operation ID reused with 
different payload or user' });
        }
      }
    
      // Execute in transaction
      const tx = db.transaction(() => {
        // Reload user to get latest authoritative revision
        const freshUser = db.prepare('SELECT id, email, name, initials, 
highest_level, best_score, revision FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(user.id);
        const activeRunRow = db.prepare('SELECT run_id, state_json, updated_at 
FROM active_runs WHERE user_id = ?').get(user.id);
        const authoritativeRun = activeRunRow ? 
JSON.parse(activeRunRow.state_json) : null;
    
        if (freshUser.revision !== expected_revision) {
          const conflictBody = {
            error: 'Stale revision conflict',
            current_revision: freshUser.revision,
            expected_revision: expected_revision,
            active_run: authoritativeRun,
            user: {
              id: freshUser.id,
              email: freshUser.email,
              name: freshUser.name,
              initials: freshUser.initials,
              highest_level: freshUser.highest_level,
              best_score: freshUser.best_score,
              revision: freshUser.revision
            }
          };
    
          db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO operation_receipts (operation_id, user_id, request_hash,
status_code, response_body, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
          `).run(operation_id, freshUser.id, payloadHash, 409, 
JSON.stringify(conflictBody), new Date().toISOString());
    
          return { status: 409, body: conflictBody };
        }
    
        // Advance revision
        const newRevision = freshUser.revision + 1;
        db.prepare('UPDATE users SET revision = ? WHERE id = 
?').run(newRevision, freshUser.id);
    
        // Run specific mutation logic
        const mutationResult = mutationHandler(db, freshUser, newRevision, 
req.body);
    
        const successBody = Object.assign({
          success: true,
          revision: newRevision
        }, mutationResult);
    
        db.prepare(`
          INSERT INTO operation_receipts (operation_id, user_id, request_hash, 
status_code, response_body, created_at)
          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `).run(operation_id, freshUser.id, payloadHash, 200, 
JSON.stringify(successBody), new Date().toISOString());
    
        return { status: 200, body: successBody };
      });
    
      try {
        const result = tx();
        return res.status(result.status).json(result.body);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Mutation error:', err);
        return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error during 
mutation' });
      }
    }
    
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // Authentication Endpoints
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    app.post('/api/auth/login', (req, res) => {
      const { email, password } = req.body || {};
      if (!email || !password) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email and password are required' 
});
      }
    
      const user = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = 
?').get(String(email).trim().toLowerCase());
      if (!user) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password' });
      }
    
      const hash = hashPassword(String(password), user.salt);
      if (hash !== user.password_hash) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password' });
      }
    
      // 64-character lowercase hexadecimal bearer token
      const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
      db.prepare('INSERT INTO auth_tokens (token, user_id, created_at) VALUES 
(?, ?, ?)').run(
        token,
        user.id,
        new Date().toISOString()
      );
    
      const activeRunRow = db.prepare('SELECT run_id, state_json, updated_at 
FROM active_runs WHERE user_id = ?').get(user.id);
      const activeRun = activeRunRow ? JSON.parse(activeRunRow.state_json) : 
null;
    
      return res.json({
        token,
        user: {
          id: user.id,
          email: user.email,
          name: user.name,
          initials: user.initials,
          highest_level: user.highest_level,
          best_score: user.best_score,
          revision: user.revision
        },
        active_run: activeRun
      });
    });
    
    app.post('/api/auth/logout', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      // Revoke all active tokens for this user
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM auth_tokens WHERE user_id = ?').run(req.user.id);
      return res.json({ success: true });
    });
    
    app.get('/api/auth/me', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      const freshUser = db.prepare('SELECT id, email, name, initials, 
highest_level, best_score, revision FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(req.user.id);
      const activeRunRow = db.prepare('SELECT run_id, state_json, updated_at 
FROM active_runs WHERE user_id = ?').get(req.user.id);
      const activeRun = activeRunRow ? JSON.parse(activeRunRow.state_json) : 
null;
    
      return res.json({
        user: freshUser,
        active_run: activeRun
      });
    });
    
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // Game Data & Config Endpoints
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    app.get('/api/levels', (req, res) => {
      const levels = db.prepare('SELECT level, name, base_speed, speed_cap, 
accent, digest FROM levels ORDER BY level ASC').all();
      const allBricks = db.prepare('SELECT id, level, row, column, type, "drop" 
FROM bricks ORDER BY level ASC, row ASC, column ASC').all();
    
      const enrichedLevels = levels.map(lvl => {
        const lvlBricks = allBricks.filter(b => b.level === lvl.level);
        const brickTotals = {
          normal: lvlBricks.filter(b => b.type === 'normal').length,
          strong: lvlBricks.filter(b => b.type === 'strong').length,
          solid: lvlBricks.filter(b => b.type === 'solid').length,
          total: lvlBricks.length
        };
        return Object.assign({}, lvl, {
          bricks: lvlBricks,
          brick_totals: brickTotals
        });
      });
    
      return res.json({ levels: enrichedLevels });
    });
    
    app.get('/api/constants', (req, res) => {
      const rows = db.prepare('SELECT key, value FROM constants').all();
      return res.json({ constants: rows });
    });
    
    app.get('/api/leaderboard', (req, res) => {
      const rows = db.prepare('SELECT initials, score, level, achieved_at FROM 
leaderboard ORDER BY score DESC, achieved_at ASC LIMIT 10').all();
      return res.json({ leaderboard: rows });
    });
    
    app.get('/api/drills', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      return res.json({ drills: DRILL_SUMMARIES });
    });
    
    app.get('/api/drills/:id', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      const drill = createDrillState(req.params.id);
      if (!drill) {
        return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Drill not found' });
      }
      return res.json({ drill });
    });
    
    app.get('/api/runs', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      const rows = db.prepare('SELECT run_id, outcome, level, score, 
finished_at, snapshot_json FROM personal_runs WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY 
finished_at DESC LIMIT 10').all();
      const runs = rows.map(r => ({
        run_id: r.run_id,
        outcome: r.outcome,
        level: r.level,
        score: r.score,
        finished_at: r.finished_at,
        snapshot: JSON.parse(r.snapshot_json)
      }));
      return res.json({ runs });
    });
    
    app.get('/api/runs/:run_id', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      const row = db.prepare('SELECT run_id, outcome, level, score, finished_at,
snapshot_json FROM personal_runs WHERE user_id = ? AND run_id = 
?').get(req.user.id, req.params.run_id);
      if (!row) {
        return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Run record not found' });
      }
      return res.json({
        run: {
          run_id: row.run_id,
          outcome: row.outcome,
          level: row.level,
          score: row.score,
          finished_at: row.finished_at,
          snapshot: JSON.parse(row.snapshot_json)
        }
      });
    });
    
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // Protected Game Mutation Endpoints
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    app.post('/api/game/start', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      executeGameMutation(req, res, (database, user, newRevision, body) => {
        const { level, run_id } = body;
        const chosenLevel = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(level) || 1, 
user.highest_level));
        const runId = run_id || 
`run_${Date.now()}_${crypto.randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}`;
    
        const levelData = database.prepare('SELECT * FROM levels WHERE level = 
?').get(chosenLevel);
        const brickRows = database.prepare('SELECT id, level, row, column, type,
"drop" FROM bricks WHERE level = ?').all(chosenLevel);
    
        const initialState = {
          run_id: runId,
          status: 'ready',
          level: chosenLevel,
          score: 0,
          lives: CONSTANTS.INITIAL_LIVES,
          combo: 1,
          next_extra_life: CONSTANTS.EXTRA_LIFE_STEP,
          paddle: {
            x: (CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH - CONSTANTS.PADDLE_WIDTH) / 2,
            y: CONSTANTS.PADDLE_Y,
            width: CONSTANTS.PADDLE_WIDTH,
            height: CONSTANTS.PADDLE_HEIGHT,
            vx: 0
          },
          paddle_width: CONSTANTS.PADDLE_WIDTH,
          power: null,
          power_seconds: 0,
          baseSpeed: levelData ? levelData.base_speed : 300,
          speedCap: levelData ? levelData.speed_cap : 520,
          balls: [
            {
              id: 'primary',
              isPrimary: true,
              x: CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH / 2,
              y: CONSTANTS.PADDLE_Y - CONSTANTS.BALL_RADIUS,
              vx: 0,
              vy: 0,
              radius: CONSTANTS.BALL_RADIUS,
              held: true,
              heldOffset: 0,
              lost: false
            }
          ],
          drops: [],
          bricks: brickRows.map(b => ({
            row: b.row,
            column: b.column,
            type: b.type,
            hp: b.type === 'strong' ? 2 : (b.type === 'normal' ? 1 : Infinity),
            drop: b.drop || ''
          }))
        };
    
        database.prepare(`
          INSERT OR REPLACE INTO active_runs (user_id, run_id, state_json, 
updated_at)
          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
        `).run(user.id, runId, JSON.stringify(initialState), new 
Date().toISOString());
    
        return {
          active_run: initialState
        };
      });
    });
    
    app.post('/api/game/save', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      executeGameMutation(req, res, (database, user, newRevision, body) => {
        const { run_id, state } = body;
        if (!state) {
          return {};
        }
    
        database.prepare(`
          INSERT OR REPLACE INTO active_runs (user_id, run_id, state_json, 
updated_at)
          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
        `).run(user.id, run_id || state.run_id || 'active', 
JSON.stringify(state), new Date().toISOString());
    
        return { saved: true };
      });
    });
    
    app.post('/api/game/progress', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      executeGameMutation(req, res, (database, user, newRevision, body) => {
        const { completed_level, score, next_level, state } = body;
        const completedLvl = Number(completed_level) || 1;
        const currentScore = Number(score) || 0;
    
        let newHighestLevel = user.highest_level;
        if (completedLvl >= user.highest_level && completedLvl < 10) {
          newHighestLevel = completedLvl + 1;
          database.prepare('UPDATE users SET highest_level = ? WHERE id = 
?').run(newHighestLevel, user.id);
        }
    
        let newBestScore = user.best_score;
        if (currentScore > user.best_score) {
          newBestScore = currentScore;
          database.prepare('UPDATE users SET best_score = ? WHERE id = 
?').run(newBestScore, user.id);
        }
    
        if (state) {
          database.prepare(`
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO active_runs (user_id, run_id, state_json, 
updated_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
          `).run(user.id, state.run_id || 'active', JSON.stringify(state), new 
Date().toISOString());
        }
    
        return {
          highest_level: newHighestLevel,
          best_score: newBestScore
        };
      });
    });
    
    app.post('/api/game/finish', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      executeGameMutation(req, res, (database, user, newRevision, body) => {
        const { run_id, outcome, level, score, snapshot } = body;
        const finalScore = Number(score) || 0;
        const finalLevel = Number(level) || 1;
        const finalOutcome = outcome === 'completed' ? 'completed' : 
'game-over';
        const finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    
        // Check if this run_id was already recorded
        const existingPersonal = database.prepare('SELECT id FROM personal_runs 
WHERE user_id = ? AND run_id = ?').get(user.id, run_id);
        if (!existingPersonal) {
          // Insert personal run
          database.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO personal_runs (user_id, run_id, outcome, level, score, 
finished_at, snapshot_json)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
          `).run(
            user.id,
            String(run_id),
            finalOutcome,
            finalLevel,
            finalScore,
            finishedAt,
            JSON.stringify(snapshot || {})
          );
    
          // Keep only newest 10 personal runs
          database.prepare(`
            DELETE FROM personal_runs
            WHERE user_id = ?
            AND id NOT IN (
              SELECT id FROM personal_runs
              WHERE user_id = ?
              ORDER BY finished_at DESC, id DESC
              LIMIT 10
            )
          `).run(user.id, user.id);
    
          // Insert into global leaderboard
          database.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO leaderboard (user_id, initials, score, level, 
achieved_at, run_id)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
          `).run(
            user.id,
            user.initials,
            finalScore,
            finalLevel,
            finishedAt,
            String(run_id)
          );
        }
    
        // Update best score
        let newBestScore = user.best_score;
        if (finalScore > user.best_score) {
          newBestScore = finalScore;
          database.prepare('UPDATE users SET best_score = ? WHERE id = 
?').run(newBestScore, user.id);
        }
    
        // Clear resumable run
        database.prepare('DELETE FROM active_runs WHERE user_id = 
?').run(user.id);
    
        // Fetch top 10 leaderboard
        const top10 = database.prepare('SELECT initials, score, level, 
achieved_at FROM leaderboard ORDER BY score DESC, achieved_at ASC LIMIT 
10').all();
    
        return {
          best_score: newBestScore,
          leaderboard: top10
        };
      });
    });
    
    app.post('/api/game/clear', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      executeGameMutation(req, res, (database, user, newRevision, body) => {
        database.prepare('DELETE FROM active_runs WHERE user_id = 
?').run(user.id);
        return { cleared: true };
      });
    });
    
    // Fallback: serve index.html for client-side navigation
    app.get('*', (req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });
    
    // Start server
    if (require.main === module) {
      app.listen(PORT, () => {
        console.log(`Brickfall arcade server running on 
http://localhost:${PORT}`);
      });
    }
    
    module.exports = app;

+121|    return res.status(result.status).json(result.body);

+269|app.post('/api/game/start', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
+270|  executeGameMutation(req, res, (database, user, newRevision, body) => {
+271|    const { level, run_id } = body;
+272|    const chosenLevel = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(level) || 1, 
user.highest_level));
+273|    const runId = run_id || 
`run_${Date.now()}_${crypto.randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}`;
+274|
+275|    const levelData = database.prepare('SELECT * FROM levels WHERE level = 
?').get(chosenLevel);
+276|    const brickRows = database.prepare('SELECT id, level, row, column, 
type, "drop" FROM bricks WHERE level = ?').all(chosenLevel);
+277|
+278|    const initialState = {
+279|      run_id: runId,
+280|      status: 'ready',
+281|      level: chosenLevel,
+282|      score: 0,
+283|      lives: CONSTANTS.INITIAL_LIVES,
+284|      combo: 1,
+285|      next_extra_life: CONSTANTS.EXTRA_LIFE_STEP,
+286|      paddle: {
+287|        x: (CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH - CONSTANTS.PADDLE_WIDTH) / 2,
+288|        y: CONSTANTS.PADDLE_Y,
+289|        width: CONSTANTS.PADDLE_WIDTH,
+290|        height: CONSTANTS.PADDLE_HEIGHT,
+291|        vx: 0
+292|      },
+293|      paddle_width: CONSTANTS.PADDLE_WIDTH,
+294|      power: null,
+295|      power_seconds: 0,
+296|      baseSpeed: levelData ? levelData.base_speed : 300,
+297|      speedCap: levelData ? levelData.speed_cap : 520,
+298|      balls: [
+299|        {
+300|          id: 'primary',
+301|          isPrimary: true,
+302|          x: CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH / 2,

+304|          vx: 0,
+305|          vy: 0,
+306|          radius: CONSTANTS.BALL_RADIUS,
+307|          held: true,
+308|          heldOffset: 0,
+309|          lost: false
+310|        }
+311|      ],
+312|      drops: [],
+313|      bricks: brickRows.map(b => ({
+314|        row: b.row,
+315|        column: b.column,
+316|        type: b.type,
+317|        hp: b.type === 'strong' ? 2 : (b.type === 'normal' ? 1 : Infinity),
+318|        drop: b.drop || ''
+319|      }))
+320|    };
+321|
+322|    database.prepare(`
+323|      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO active_runs (user_id, run_id, state_json, 
updated_at)
+324|      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
+325|    `).run(user.id, runId, JSON.stringify(initialState), new 
Date().toISOString());

+328|      active_run: initialState
+329|    };
+330|  });

+349|app.post('/api/game/progress', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
+350|  executeGameMutation(req, res, (database, user, newRevision, body) => {
+351|    const { completed_level, score, next_level, state } = body;
+352|    const completedLvl = Number(completed_level) || 1;
+353|    const currentScore = Number(score) || 0;
+354|
+355|    let newHighestLevel = user.highest_level;
+356|    if (completedLvl >= user.highest_level && completedLvl < 10) {
+357|      newHighestLevel = completedLvl + 1;
+358|      database.prepare('UPDATE users SET highest_level = ? WHERE id = 
?').run(newHighestLevel, user.id);
+359|    }
+360|
+361|    let newBestScore = user.best_score;
+362|    if (currentScore > user.best_score) {
+363|      newBestScore = currentScore;
+364|      database.prepare('UPDATE users SET best_score = ? WHERE id = 
?').run(newBestScore, user.id);
+365|    }
+366|
+367|    if (state) {
+368|      database.prepare(`
+369|        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO active_runs (user_id, run_id, state_json, 
updated_at)
+370|        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
+371|      `).run(user.id, state.run_id || 'active', JSON.stringify(state), new 
Date().toISOString());
+372|    }
+373|
+374|    return {
+375|      highest_level: newHighestLevel,
+376|      best_score: newBestScore
+377|    };
+378|  });
+379|});
+380|
+381|app.post('/api/game/finish', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
+382|  executeGameMutation(req, res, (database, user, newRevision, body) => {
+383|    const { run_id, outcome, level, score, snapshot } = body;
+384|    const finalScore = Number(score) || 0;
+385|    const finalLevel = Number(level) || 1;
+386|    const finalOutcome = outcome === 'completed' ? 'completed' : 
'game-over';
+387|    const finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
+388|
+389|    // Check if this run_id was already recorded
+390|    const existingPersonal = database.prepare('SELECT id FROM personal_runs
WHERE user_id = ? AND run_id = ?').get(user.id, run_id);
+391|    if (!existingPersonal) {
+392|      // Insert personal run
+393|      database.prepare(`
+394|        INSERT INTO personal_runs (user_id, run_id, outcome, level, score, 
finished_at, snapshot_json)
+395|        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+396|      `).run(
+397|        user.id,
+398|        String(run_id),
+399|        finalOutcome,
+400|        finalLevel,
+401|        finalScore,
+402|        finishedAt,
+403|        JSON.stringify(snapshot || {})
+404|      );
+405|
+406|      // Keep only newest 10 personal runs
+407|      database.prepare(`
+408|        DELETE FROM personal_runs
+409|        WHERE user_id = ?
+410|        AND id NOT IN (
+411|          SELECT id FROM personal_runs
+412|          WHERE user_id = ?
+413|          ORDER BY finished_at DESC, id DESC
+414|          LIMIT 10
+415|        )
+416|      `).run(user.id, user.id);
+417|
+418|      // Insert into global leaderboard
+419|      database.prepare(`
+420|        INSERT INTO leaderboard (user_id, initials, score, level, 
achieved_at, run_id)
+421|        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+422|      `).run(
+423|        user.id,
+424|        user.initials,
+425|        finalScore,
+426|        finalLevel,
+427|        finishedAt,
+428|        String(run_id)
+429|      );
+430|    }
+431|
+432|    // Update best score
+433|    let newBestScore = user.best_score;
+434|    if (finalScore > user.best_score) {
+435|      newBestScore = finalScore;
+436|      database.prepare('UPDATE users SET best_score = ? WHERE id = 
?').run(newBestScore, user.id);
+437|    }
+438|
+439|    // Clear resumable run
+440|    database.prepare('DELETE FROM active_runs WHERE user_id = 
?').run(user.id);
+441|
+442|    // Fetch top 10 leaderboard
+443|    const top10 = database.prepare('SELECT initials, score, level, 
achieved_at FROM leaderboard ORDER BY score DESC, achieved_at ASC LIMIT 
10').all();
+444|
+445|    return {
+446|      best_score: newBestScore,
+447|      leaderboard: top10
+448|    };
+449|  });
+450|});
+451|
+452|app.post('/api/game/clear', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
+453|  executeGameMutation(req, res, (database, user, newRevision, body) => {
+454|    database.prepare('DELETE FROM active_runs WHERE user_id = 
?').run(user.id);
+455|    return { cleared: true };
+456|  });
+457|});

+459|// Fallback: serve index.html for client-side navigation

Reasoning:
**Automated API Testing Script - My Approach**

Okay, so I need to thoroughly test this server and its API endpoints. My plan is
to write a Node.js test script to automate the process. This is the only way to 
ensure the robustness and reliability of the system, I know. I want to cover all
bases.

First, I'll focus on authentication. I need to test login for Mira, Dev, and 
Polly, using "password123". After successful logins, I'll verify proper token 
generation and authorization.

Next up, I'll need to check the core API functionality. I'll hit the following 
endpoints: `/api/auth/me`, `/api/leaderboard`, `/api/levels`, `/api/constants`, 
`/api/drills`, and `/api/runs`. Making sure they return the expected data and 
behave as intended.

Idempotency is crucial for data integrity, so I'll specifically test that. I'll 
make sure that replaying an operation with the same `operation_id` and the same 
payload produces the exact same response. Then, I'll check that a replay with a 
*different* payload results in a 400 error. That's good practice.

Revision checking is another important aspect. I'll verify that sending an 
incorrect `expected_revision` results in a 409 Conflict, ensuring data 
consistency and reflecting the server's state. When the correct 
`expected_revision` is sent, I expect the revision to advance accordingly.

Finally, I need to go through the finishing a run workflow. This is key. The 
tests need to verify that the leaderboard updates correctly. I need to ensure 
personal runs are stored, keeping the max of 10 runs, as designed. The tests 
have to clear any active runs and crucially, ensure that retrying a "finish" 
doesn't duplicate leaderboard entries. That's a classic gotcha.

Overall, it's clear what I have to do. I need to write and run this automated 
API test script to confidently validate the system.




$ node -e "
const http = require('http');
const app = require('/app/server.js');

const server = app.listen(0, async () => {
  const port = server.address().port;
  console.log('Test server running on port', port);

  async function req(path, options = {}) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const opt = Object.assign({
        hostname: '127.0.0.1',
        port: port,
        path: path,
        method: 'GET',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
      }, options);

      const request = http.request(opt, res => {
        let data = '';
        res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
        res.on('end', () => {
          let json = null;
          try { json = JSON.parse(data); } catch (e) { json = data; }
          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, data: json });
        });
      });
      request.on('error', reject);
      if (options.body) {
        request.write(typeof options.body === 'string' ? options.body : 
JSON.stringify(options.body));
      }
      request.end();
    });
  }

  try {
    console.log('--- TEST 1: Login Polly ---');
    const loginRes = await req('/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: { email: 'polly@brickfall.test', password: 'password123' }
    });
    console.log('Login status:', loginRes.status, 'User:', 
loginRes.data.user.email, 'Token len:', loginRes.data.token.length);
    const token = loginRes.data.token;
    const authHeaders = { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token };

    console.log('--- TEST 2: /api/auth/me ---');
    const meRes = await req('/api/auth/me', { headers: authHeaders });
    console.log('Me status:', meRes.status, 'Initials:', 
meRes.data.user.initials, 'Revision:', meRes.data.user.revision);

    console.log('--- TEST 3: Idempotency & Revision ---');
    const op1 = 'op-test-123';
    const startRes1 = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: meRes.data.user.revision, operation_id: op1, 
level: 1 }
    });
    console.log('Start 1 status:', startRes1.status, 'New revision:', 
startRes1.data.revision);

    // Replay same op id & payload
    const startResReplay = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: meRes.data.user.revision, operation_id: op1, 
level: 1 }
    });
    console.log('Start replay status:', startResReplay.status, 'Revision:', 
startResReplay.data.revision);

    // Replay same op id with different payload -> 400
    const startResDiff = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: meRes.data.user.revision, operation_id: op1, 
level: 2 }
    });
    console.log('Start diff payload status:', startResDiff.status, 'Expected: 
400');

    // Stale revision test -> 409
    const staleRes = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: 999, operation_id: 'op-stale-999', level: 1 }
    });
    console.log('Stale revision status:', staleRes.status, 'Expected: 409', 
'Current rev:', staleRes.data.current_revision);

    console.log('--- TEST 4: Finish run & idempotency ---');
    const finishOp = 'op-finish-1';
    const finishRes = await req('/api/game/finish', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: {
        expected_revision: startRes1.data.revision,
        operation_id: finishOp,
        run_id: 'test-run-polly-1',
        outcome: 'completed',
        level: 10,
        score: 35000,
        snapshot: { score: 35000 }
      }
    });
    console.log('Finish status:', finishRes.status, 'Best score:', 
finishRes.data.best_score, 'Leaderboard top 1:', finishRes.data.leaderboard[0]);

    console.log('--- TEST 5: Logout ---');
    const logoutRes = await req('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST', headers: 
authHeaders });
    console.log('Logout status:', logoutRes.status);
    const postLogout = await req('/api/auth/me', { headers: authHeaders });
    console.log('Post logout /api/auth/me status:', postLogout.status, 
'Expected: 401');

    console.log('ALL TESTS PASSED!');
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Test failed:', err);
  } finally {
    server.close();
  }
});

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Use fallback middleware in Express 5

Reasoning:
**Refactoring Express Wildcard Route Handling**

Okay, so I'm looking at this Express application using `path-to-regexp` v8, and 
the wildcard route is currently handled with the traditional `app.get('*', ...)`
construct. I know, from experience, that's not the most modern approach, and it 
can sometimes lead to unexpected behavior, especially when dealing with more 
complex routing setups. I prefer the clarity and flexibility of the `/{*splat}` 
syntax for wildcard routes, or even better, using `app.use()` as a catch-all.

Therefore, I'm going to make a small but significant change in `/app/server.js`.
I'll replace the existing `app.get('*', ...)` route, which likely serves a 
default `index.html` file, with the more robust `app.use((req, res) => 
res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html')))` pattern. This 
`app.use()` call will act as a last resort, catching any requests that haven't 
been handled by more specific routes and serving the `index.html` file in the 
`public` directory. This is not only cleaner but can improve the overall flow of
the application. The benefits are consistency with updated best practices, as 
well as the improved organization in larger, more complex applications.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "str_replace"
  path: "/app/server.js"
  old_str: 
    // Fallback: serve index.html for client-side navigation
    app.get('*', (req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });

  new_str: 
    // Fallback: serve index.html for client-side navigation
    app.use((req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

const http = require('http');
const app = require('/app/server.js');

const server = app.listen(0, async () => {
  const port = server.address().port;
  console.log('Test server running on port', port);

  async function req(path, options = {}) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const opt = Object.assign({
        hostname: '127.0.0.1',
        port: port,
        path: path,
        method: 'GET',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
      }, options);

      const request = http.request(opt, res => {
        let data = '';
        res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
        res.on('end', () => {
          let json = null;
          try { json = JSON.parse(data); } catch (e) { json = data; }
          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, data: json });
        });
      });
      request.on('error', reject);
      if (options.body) {
        request.write(typeof options.body === 'string' ? options.body : 
JSON.stringify(options.body));
      }
      request.end();
    });
  }

  try {
    console.log('--- TEST 1: Login Polly ---');
    const loginRes = await req('/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: { email: 'polly@brickfall.test', password: 'password123' }
    });
    console.log('Login status:', loginRes.status, 'User:', 
loginRes.data.user.email, 'Token len:', loginRes.data.token.length);
    const token = loginRes.data.token;
    const authHeaders = { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token };

    console.log('--- TEST 2: /api/auth/me ---');
    const meRes = await req('/api/auth/me', { headers: authHeaders });
    console.log('Me status:', meRes.status, 'Initials:', 
meRes.data.user.initials, 'Revision:', meRes.data.user.revision);

    console.log('--- TEST 3: Idempotency & Revision ---');
    const op1 = 'op-test-123';
    const startRes1 = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: meRes.data.user.revision, operation_id: op1, 
level: 1 }
    });
    console.log('Start 1 status:', startRes1.status, 'New revision:', 
startRes1.data.revision);

    // Replay same op id & payload
    const startResReplay = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: meRes.data.user.revision, operation_id: op1, 
level: 1 }
    });
    console.log('Start replay status:', startResReplay.status, 'Revision:', 
startResReplay.data.revision);

    // Replay same op id with different payload -> 400
    const startResDiff = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: meRes.data.user.revision, operation_id: op1, 
level: 2 }
    });
    console.log('Start diff payload status:', startResDiff.status, 'Expected: 
400');

    // Stale revision test -> 409
    const staleRes = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: 999, operation_id: 'op-stale-999', level: 1 }
    });
    console.log('Stale revision status:', staleRes.status, 'Expected: 409', 
'Current rev:', staleRes.data.current_revision);

    console.log('--- TEST 4: Finish run & idempotency ---');
    const finishOp = 'op-finish-1';
    const finishRes = await req('/api/game/finish', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: {
        expected_revision: startRes1.data.revision,
        operation_id: finishOp,
        run_id: 'test-run-polly-1',
        outcome: 'completed',
        level: 10,
        score: 35000,
        snapshot: { score: 35000 }
      }
    });
    console.log('Finish status:', finishRes.status, 'Best score:', 
finishRes.data.best_score, 'Leaderboard top 1:', finishRes.data.leaderboard[0]);

    console.log('--- TEST 5: Logout ---');
    const logoutRes = await req('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST', headers: 
authHeaders });
    console.log('Logout status:', logoutRes.status);
    const postLogout = await req('/api/auth/me', { headers: authHeaders });
    console.log('Post logout /api/auth/me status:', postLogout.status, 
'Expected: 401');

    console.log('ALL TESTS PASSED!');
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Test failed:', err);
  } finally {
    server.close();
  }
});
"

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Add fallback for req.body in executeGameMutation

Reasoning:
**Debugging a Header Overwrite**

Okay, let's break this down. In the test helper `req()`, I was passing `headers:
authHeaders`, and `authHeaders` was a simple object containing the 
`Authorization` header. I see now that this completely *replaced* the default 
`headers` object, which included the crucial `Content-Type: application/json`. 
That means `express.json()` wasn't parsing the request body! I need to ensure 
the headers are merged, not overwritten. I'll change the `req()` helper to use 
`Object.assign` to merge the default `Content-Type` with any custom headers 
passed in: `headers: Object.assign({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
options.headers)`.

Also, to be more robust, when I'm executing the game mutation, I should add a 
check for the existence of `req.body`.  I'll add `const body = req.body || {};` 
so that it handles undefined bodies gracefully. Then I'll destructure as before 
with `const { expected_revision, operation_id } = body;`. This should protect 
against null or undefined body requests!

Let's get this updated in `server.js` and run the tests to confirm the fix.

      const user = req.user;
      const body = req.body || {};

        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Missing or invalid operation_id'

const http = require('http');
const app = require('/app/server.js');

const server = app.listen(0, async () => {
  const port = server.address().port;
  console.log('Test server running on port', port);

  async function req(path, options = {}) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const opt = Object.assign({
        hostname: '127.0.0.1',
        port: port,
        path: path,
        method: 'GET'
      }, options);
      opt.headers = Object.assign({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
options.headers || {});

      const request = http.request(opt, res => {
        let data = '';
        res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
        res.on('end', () => {
          let json = null;
          try { json = JSON.parse(data); } catch (e) { json = data; }
          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, data: json });
        });
      });
      request.on('error', reject);
      if (options.body) {
        request.write(typeof options.body === 'string' ? options.body : 
JSON.stringify(options.body));
      }
      request.end();
    });
  }

  try {
    console.log('--- TEST 1: Login Polly ---');
    const loginRes = await req('/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: { email: 'polly@brickfall.test', password: 'password123' }
    });
    console.log('Login status:', loginRes.status, 'User:', 
loginRes.data.user.email, 'Token len:', loginRes.data.token.length);
    const token = loginRes.data.token;
    const authHeaders = { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token };

    console.log('--- TEST 2: /api/auth/me ---');
    const meRes = await req('/api/auth/me', { headers: authHeaders });
    console.log('Me status:', meRes.status, 'Initials:', 
meRes.data.user.initials, 'Revision:', meRes.data.user.revision);

    console.log('--- TEST 3: Idempotency & Revision ---');
    const op1 = 'op-test-123';
    const startRes1 = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: meRes.data.user.revision, operation_id: op1, 
level: 1 }
    });
    console.log('Start 1 status:', startRes1.status, 'New revision:', 
startRes1.data.revision);

    // Replay same op id & payload
    const startResReplay = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: meRes.data.user.revision, operation_id: op1, 
level: 1 }
    });
    console.log('Start replay status:', startResReplay.status, 'Revision:', 
startResReplay.data.revision);

    // Replay same op id with different payload -> 400
    const startResDiff = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: meRes.data.user.revision, operation_id: op1, 
level: 2 }
    });
    console.log('Start diff payload status:', startResDiff.status, 'Expected: 
400');

    // Stale revision test -> 409
    const staleRes = await req('/api/game/start', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: { expected_revision: 999, operation_id: 'op-stale-999', level: 1 }
    });
    console.log('Stale revision status:', staleRes.status, 'Expected: 409', 
'Current rev:', staleRes.data.current_revision);

    console.log('--- TEST 4: Finish run & idempotency ---');
    const finishOp = 'op-finish-1';
    const finishRes = await req('/api/game/finish', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders,
      body: {
        expected_revision: startRes1.data.revision,
        operation_id: finishOp,
        run_id: 'test-run-polly-1',
        outcome: 'completed',
        level: 10,
        score: 35000,
        snapshot: { score: 35000 }
      }
    });
    console.log('Finish status:', finishRes.status, 'Best score:', 
finishRes.data.best_score, 'Leaderboard top 1:', finishRes.data.leaderboard[0]);

    console.log('--- TEST 5: Logout ---');
    const logoutRes = await req('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST', headers: 
authHeaders });
    console.log('Logout status:', logoutRes.status);
    const postLogout = await req('/api/auth/me', { headers: authHeaders });
    console.log('Post logout /api/auth/me status:', postLogout.status, 
'Expected: 401');

    console.log('ALL TESTS PASSED!');
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Test failed:', err);
  } finally {
    server.close();
  }
});
