Summary: Create /app/public/index.html semantic structure

Reasoning:
**Frontend Client Design: A Deep Dive**

Okay, time to dive into the frontend client. I need to make sure this is 
rock-solid. We're talking about crafting `/app/public/index.html`, 
`/app/public/styles.css`, and `/app/public/game.js` into something beautiful and
effective.

First, the *interface specifications* from `interface.md`:  The goal is a 
cohesive arcade theme, responsive and accessible.  The canvas needs to be the 
star, with clear visuals for everything – bricks, damage states, balls, paddle, 
the works.  Keep the essential player state (score, lives, level, etc.) outside 
of the canvas in the DOM, and make sure that essential event messages are 
clearly visible. The canvas should be keyboard accessible and the game needs to 
be fully functional on a 375-pixel screen. This means strong focus states, 
keyboard operability, touch targets, and reduced animation durations for 
performance. Assist paddle accessibility is crucial too.

Next, I need to nail the DOM structure. This is the backbone of the client. I'm 
thinking:

*   **Header:** Game title, player profile badge (name, email, initials, and 
sign-out), sync state and revision badge, and a Mechanics Lab toggle.
*   **Main Arcade Area:** The game canvas, a HUD panel with the game state, and 
a controls toolbar, including launch, pause, restart, assist paddle toggle, and 
level selection. An event message banner with a recent events list should be 
included, along with a keyboard shortcuts guide.
*   **Sign-In Modal:** Clean sign-in with a sample user and error/status alerts.
*   **Mechanics Lab Modal:** This is for deeper testing, levels overview with 
SHA-256 digest, constant tables, drill selections, and mechanics telemetry. This
includes drill loading with the ability to advance simulation, launch button for
testing sticky-catch mechanics, and a telemetry section with essential game 
variables.
*   **Run History & Leaderboard Panels:** The history and leaderboard should be 
available.

From a *canvas rendering* perspective:

*   **Bricks:** Crisp renders are a must.  Normal bricks with sleek patterns, 
strong bricks with damage indicators, and solid bricks that are obviously 
indestructible (metallic with hazard stripes).
*   **Ball:** A glowing neon orb with a crisp outline.
*   **Paddle:** Futuristic metallic design with a glowing center line.
*   **Falling Drops:** Pill capsules with clear icons.
*   **Overlays:** Clear game state messages ("READY," "PAUSED," etc.)

*Touch/pointer controls* should be intuitive:

*   Paddle control via canvas pointer drag or on-screen buttons.
*   Mobile screens should have virtual buttons.

Finally, *mobile responsiveness* is key:

*   CSS flexbox/grid with media queries for smaller screens.
*   Responsive canvas scaling (`max-width: 100%`, `aspect-ratio`).
*   Touch targets large enough to be usable, no horizontal scrolling, and good 
accessibility.
*   High contrast and distinct outlines for accessibility.

Now, let's start actually writing `/app/public/index.html`!

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Add routes for physics.js and drills.js in server.js

Reasoning:
**Ensuring Physics and Drills Scripts are Accessible**

Okay, so looking at `index.html`, I see we're including `<script 
src="physics.js"></script>` and `<script src="drills.js"></script>`. My first 
thought is to verify that these files, specifically `/app/public/physics.js` and
`/app/public/drills.js`, are actually being served correctly. Thankfully, 
`server.js` uses `express.static(path.join(__dirname, 'public'))`, which takes 
care of serving anything under `/app/public`.

So, the simplest solution is to make sure those files *are* under 
`/app/public/`. I could symlink, copy the files there, or even serve them 
directly.

But, I think adding explicit express routes is the cleanest and most reliable 
approach. This guarantees that we're serving exactly the files we intend, 
regardless of what other changes might happen to the directory structure. So, 
let's go with adding specific routes in `server.js`:

```javascript
app.get('/physics.js', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 
'physics.js')));
app.get('/drills.js', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 
'drills.js')));
```

That way, we can be absolutely certain that `/physics.js` and `/drills.js` will 
always be accessible, both server-side in Node and client-side in the browser. 
Best practice here is to get it done now. Let's update `server.js` with these 
routes right away.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "str_replace"
  path: "/app/server.js"
  old_str: 
    // Middleware
    app.use(express.json({ limit: '2mb' }));
    app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, 'public')));

  new_str: 
    // Middleware
    app.use(express.json({ limit: '2mb' }));
    app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, 'public')));
    
    // Serve physics and drills shared modules
    app.get('/physics.js', (req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'physics.js'));
    });
    app.get('/drills.js', (req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'drills.js'));
    });

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create /app/public/styles.css arcade stylesheet

Reasoning:
**Crafting a Cyberpunk Arcade Aesthetic**

Okay, time to build `/app/public/styles.css`. I'm envisioning a really sharp, 
modern arcade cyberpunk vibe here. We're going for high contrast, crisp colors, 
and this design *has* to be responsive, scaling down beautifully even on a 375px
viewport.

The brick styles are key. Normal bricks are going to pop with bright neon cyan, 
green, and yellow – clean borders, nothing distracting. Strong bricks need to 
visually convey resilience; I'm thinking thicker borders and maybe even internal
shield graphics or some cool cracked stripes to indicate damage. Solid bricks, 
on the other hand, should have a more utilitarian feel: metallic grey or slate, 
with diagonal hazard stripes to give that industrial feel.

And the canvas itself? Responsive scaling is crucial. `max-width: 100%` is a 
must, and we'll maintain that important 900:600 aspect ratio.

Accessibility is paramount, so I'll make sure there are clear, accessible focus 
outlines and minimum 44px touch targets for mobile users.

Finally, the Mechanics Lab and Snapshot inspector modals need to be clean and 
intuitive. Also, I don't want any public-internet dependencies; we'll stick to 
standard system fonts – monospace for code sections, sans-serif for general 
text, and maybe find some nice arcade-style fonts for headings. We're keeping it
lean. Now, let's write `/app/public/styles.css`.

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create /app/public/game.js client application

Reasoning:
**Game.js Design and Implementation**

Okay, let's get down to designing and coding `/app/public/game.js`.  I need to 
make sure this is well-structured and handles everything required.

First, I'll focus on **State & Persistence**.  The bearer token will live in 
either `sessionStorage` or `localStorage` under `brickfall_token`. On load, if 
it's there, I'll hit `/api/auth/me`. A 401 response means I clear the token and 
show the sign-in screen. User profile data (`id`, `email`, `name`, `initials`, 
`highest_level`, `best_score`, `revision`) will be crucial for the gameplay 
experience.  I'll need to restore the `activeRun` from the server, or create a 
new one if necessary. I'm going to carefully track the monotonic `revision` and 
keep it synchronized with the server – and I'll need a way to generate unique 
operation IDs, either with `crypto.randomUUID()` or a fallback to `randomBytes`.
A double activation suppression mechanism (`isMutating` flag) will be really 
important too.

Next, the **Game Simulation & Canvas Rendering** is key. I'll need a rock-solid 
`requestAnimationFrame` loop with a fixed sub-step, a 1/120s engine tick. I'll 
implement a bounded accumulator to handle variable `dt` from 
`requestAnimationFrame`. The canvas itself requires clear rendering, with a 
subtle arcade grid, but also distinct brick visuals. Normal bricks need their 
level accent color and crisp borders. Strong bricks demand gold/orange borders, 
shields, and maybe some cracking. Solid bricks get a dark-slate steel look. The 
paddle gets a futuristic design with a neon glow, and a wider look when the wide
powerup is active. Balls will glow, and drops are going to have their own 
glowing icons or letters. I'll make sure to update HUD elements outside the 
canvas, and use an accessible `aria-label` for screen readers.

**Input Handling & Assist Paddle** will need to be intuitive. Left/Right arrows 
and A/D keys for the paddle. Mouse or pointer control for the paddle too. Space 
key (or launch button) to launch the ball. "P" or "Escape" key for pausing. "R" 
to restart. I'll also implement touch controls for mobile. I need to make the 
*Assist Paddle* a very useful feature, but still allow player override, making 
sure that visually the toggle button and `aria-checked` are properly updated.

Then, there's **Game State Transitions & Mutations**. I need clean transitions: 
`menu` -> `ready`, `ready` -> `playing`, `playing` -> `paused`, `paused` -> 
`playing`, `life-lost`, `level-complete`, `game-over`, and the grand finale of 
`completed`. I will also need to handle the calls to the server at the end of 
each game and level. And I'll need throttled saving, calling `POST 
/api/game/save` periodically.

The **Conflict Handling & Coordination** is important. If there is a 409 
Conflict, I need to pause the active play, update my local revision and active 
run to the server's, show a sync conflict banner and re-render the engine.

The **Mechanics Lab Interface** will require a modal. Within it, the *Practice 
Drills* tab will provide selectable drills with descriptions. I need "Load 
Drill" button to reset the state, and an "Advance 1 second" button to step the 
simulation, and a "Launch" button to release the ball. This will display 
telemetry: steps, phase, score, lives, etc., including active balls and drops 
and brick states. I'll also have a *Levels & Digests* tab to show level data, 
including a SHA-256 digest, and a *Constants* tab to display game constants.

Finally, the **Run History & Leaderboard** will include a top 10 leaderboard, a 
personal run history with inspection capabilities to look at snapshots.

My goal is to craft `/app/public/game.js` cleanly, thoroughly, and with a very 
good user experience.

      function generateOpId() {
        return 'op_' + Date.now() + '_' + 
Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 11);
      }
    
      async function apiFetch(url, options = {}) {
        const headers = Object.assign({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
options.headers || {});
        if (authToken) {
          headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' + authToken;
        }
    
        const res = await fetch(url, Object.assign({}, options, { headers }));
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    
        if (res.status === 401) {
          // Discard token and show sign in
          handleSignOut();
          return { status: 401, error: 'Unauthorized', data };
        }
    
        return { status: res.status, data };
      }
    
      function setSyncStatus(state, msg = '') {
        syncDot.className = 'sync-dot ' + (state || '');
        if (state === 'conflict') {
          syncText.textContent = 'Conflict';
          syncNotice.textContent = msg || 'Sync conflict detected. Reconciled 
with server.';
          syncNotice.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else if (state === 'syncing') {
          syncText.textContent = 'Syncing...';
          syncNotice.classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
          syncText.textContent = 'Synced';
          syncNotice.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      }
    
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // Authentication Handlers
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      async function handleLogin(email, password) {
        loginError.classList.add('hidden');
        const res = await apiFetch('/api/auth/login', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
        });
    
        if (res.status === 200) {
          authToken = res.data.token;
          localStorage.setItem('brickfall_token', authToken);
          currentUser = res.data.user;
          activeRun = res.data.active_run;
          initUserSession();
        } else {
          loginError.textContent = res.data.error || 'Login failed';
          loginError.classList.remove('hidden');
        }
      }
    
      async function handleSignOut() {
        if (authToken) {
          await apiFetch('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' }).catch(() => 
{});
        }
        authToken = null;
        currentUser = null;
        activeRun = null;
        localStorage.removeItem('brickfall_token');
    
        authStatusBar.classList.add('hidden');
        gameSection.classList.add('hidden');
        authSection.classList.remove('hidden');
        labModal.classList.add('hidden');
        snapshotModal.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    
      async function checkExistingSession() {
        if (!authToken) {
          authSection.classList.remove('hidden');
          return;
        }
    
        const res = await apiFetch('/api/auth/me');
        if (res.status === 200) {
          currentUser = res.data.user;
          activeRun = res.data.active_run;
          initUserSession();
        } else {
          handleSignOut();
        }
      }
    
      function initUserSession() {
        authSection.classList.add('hidden');
        authStatusBar.classList.remove('hidden');
        gameSection.classList.remove('hidden');
    
        userInitials.textContent = currentUser.initials;
        userName.textContent = currentUser.name;
        userEmail.textContent = currentUser.email;
        revTag.textContent = `Rev #${currentUser.revision}`;
        setSyncStatus('synced');
    
        // Populate level select
        updateLevelSelect();
        loadLeaderboard();
        loadPersonalRuns();
        loadLevelsAndConstants();
    
        // Start game from active run or ready state
        if (activeRun) {
          engine = new GameEngine(activeRun);
          addEvent(`Resumed level ${engine.level} checkpoint`);
        } else {
          startNewGame(1);
        }
    
        updateUI();
      }
    
      function updateLevelSelect() {
        selectLevel.innerHTML = '';
        const maxLvl = currentUser ? currentUser.highest_level : 1;
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
          const opt = document.createElement('option');
          opt.value = i;
          opt.textContent = `Level ${i}` + (i > maxLvl ? ' (Locked)' : '');
          opt.disabled = i > maxLvl;
          if (engine && engine.level === i) opt.selected = true;
          selectLevel.appendChild(opt);
        }
      }
    
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // Game Actions & Server Coordination
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      async function startNewGame(levelNum) {
        if (isMutating) return;
        isMutating = true;
        setSyncStatus('syncing');
    
        const opId = generateOpId();
        const res = await apiFetch('/api/game/start', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({
            expected_revision: currentUser.revision,
            operation_id: opId,
            level: levelNum
          })
        });
    
        isMutating = false;
    
        if (res.status === 200) {
          currentUser.revision = res.data.revision;
          revTag.textContent = `Rev #${currentUser.revision}`;
          activeRun = res.data.active_run;
          engine = new GameEngine(activeRun);
          setSyncStatus('synced');
          addEvent(`Started Level ${levelNum}`);
          updateUI();
        } else if (res.status === 409) {
          handleConflict(res.data);
        } else {
          setSyncStatus('conflict', res.data.error);
        }
      }
    
      async function saveActiveRun() {
        if (!engine || isMutating || engine.isMechanicsLab) return;
        if (engine.status === 'game-over' || engine.status === 'completed') 
return;
    
        const opId = generateOpId();
        const snapshot = engine.getSnapshot();
    
        const res = await apiFetch('/api/game/save', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({
            expected_revision: currentUser.revision,
            operation_id: opId,
            run_id: activeRun ? activeRun.run_id : 'active',
            state: snapshot
          })
        });
    
        if (res.status === 200) {
          currentUser.revision = res.data.revision;
          revTag.textContent = `Rev #${currentUser.revision}`;
          setSyncStatus('synced');
        } else if (res.status === 409) {
          handleConflict(res.data);
        }
      }
    
      async function progressLevel() {
        if (isMutating) return;
        isMutating = true;
        setSyncStatus('syncing');
    
        const completedLvl = engine.level;
        const currentScore = engine.score;
        const opId = generateOpId();
    
        const res = await apiFetch('/api/game/progress', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({
            expected_revision: currentUser.revision,
            operation_id: opId,
            run_id: activeRun ? activeRun.run_id : 'active',
            completed_level: completedLvl,
            score: currentScore,
            next_level: Math.min(10, completedLvl + 1),
            state: engine.getSnapshot()
          })
        });
    
        isMutating = false;
    
        if (res.status === 200) {
          currentUser.revision = res.data.revision;
          currentUser.highest_level = res.data.highest_level;
          currentUser.best_score = res.data.best_score;
          revTag.textContent = `Rev #${currentUser.revision}`;
          setSyncStatus('synced');
          updateLevelSelect();
          loadLeaderboard();
    
          if (completedLvl < 10) {
            startNewGame(completedLvl + 1);
          }
        } else if (res.status === 409) {
          handleConflict(res.data);
        }
      }
    
      async function finishRun(outcome) {
        if (isMutating) return;
        isMutating = true;
        setSyncStatus('syncing');
    
        const opId = generateOpId();
        const finalScore = engine.score;
        const finalLevel = engine.level;
        const snapshot = engine.getSnapshot();
    
        const res = await apiFetch('/api/game/finish', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({
            expected_revision: currentUser.revision,
            operation_id: opId,
            run_id: activeRun ? activeRun.run_id : `run_${Date.now()}`,
            outcome: outcome,
            level: finalLevel,
            score: finalScore,
            snapshot: snapshot
          })
        });
    
        isMutating = false;
    
        if (res.status === 200) {
          currentUser.revision = res.data.revision;
          currentUser.best_score = res.data.best_score;
          revTag.textContent = `Rev #${currentUser.revision}`;
          activeRun = null;
          setSyncStatus('synced');
          renderLeaderboardTable(res.data.leaderboard);
          loadPersonalRuns();
        } else if (res.status === 409) {
          handleConflict(res.data);
        }
      }
    
      function handleConflict(conflictData) {
        currentUser.revision = conflictData.current_revision;
        revTag.textContent = `Rev #${currentUser.revision}`;
    
        if (conflictData.active_run) {
          activeRun = conflictData.active_run;
          engine = new GameEngine(activeRun);
          engine.status = 'paused';
        }
    
        setSyncStatus('conflict', `Sync conflict: Server revision 
${conflictData.current_revision} won. Game paused and reconciled.`);
        addEvent(`Conflict reconciled: Server revision 
#${conflictData.current_revision}`);
        updateUI();
      }
    
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // Leaderboard & Personal Runs
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      async function loadLeaderboard() {
        const res = await apiFetch('/api/leaderboard');
        if (res.status === 200) {
          renderLeaderboardTable(res.data.leaderboard);
        }
      }
    
      function renderLeaderboardTable(rows) {
        leaderboardTbody.innerHTML = '';
        (rows || []).forEach((r, idx) => {
          const tr = document.createElement('tr');
          const d = r.achieved_at ? new Date(r.achieved_at).toLocaleDateString()
: 'N/A';
          tr.innerHTML = `
            <td><strong>#${idx + 1}</strong></td>
            <td><span class="initials-avatar" 
style="font-size:0.65rem;padding:2px 4px;">${r.initials}</span></td>
            <td><strong>${r.score.toLocaleString()}</strong></td>
            <td>Lvl ${r.level}</td>
            <td><small>${d}</small></td>
          `;
          leaderboardTbody.appendChild(tr);
        });
      }
    
      async function loadPersonalRuns() {
        const res = await apiFetch('/api/runs');
        if (res.status === 200) {
          renderPersonalRunsTable(res.data.runs);
        }
      }
    
      function renderPersonalRunsTable(runs) {
        runsTbody.innerHTML = '';
        (runs || []).forEach(r => {
          const tr = document.createElement('tr');
          const d = r.finished_at ? new Date(r.finished_at).toLocaleDateString()
: 'N/A';
          const outcomeBadge = r.outcome === 'completed'
            ? '<span 
style="color:var(--accent-green);font-weight:700;">Completed</span>'
            : '<span style="color:var(--accent-red);">Game Over</span>';
    
          tr.innerHTML = `
            <td>${outcomeBadge}</td>
            <td><strong>${r.score.toLocaleString()}</strong></td>
            <td>Lvl ${r.level}</td>
            <td><small>${d}</small></td>
            <td><button type="button" class="btn btn-xs btn-outline 
btn-inspect-run" data-runid="${r.run_id}">Inspect</button></td>
          `;
          runsTbody.appendChild(tr);
        });
    
        // Add inspect event listeners
        document.querySelectorAll('.btn-inspect-run').forEach(btn => {
          btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const runId = btn.getAttribute('data-runid');
            const res = await apiFetch(`/api/runs/${runId}`);
            if (res.status === 200) {
              snapshotJson.textContent = JSON.stringify(res.data.run, null, 2);
              snapshotModal.classList.remove('hidden');
            }
          });
        });
      }
    
      async function loadLevelsAndConstants() {
        const [lvlRes, constRes, drillRes] = await Promise.all([
          apiFetch('/api/levels'),
          apiFetch('/api/constants'),
          apiFetch('/api/drills')
        ]);
    
        if (lvlRes.status === 200) {
          levelsData = lvlRes.data.levels;
          renderLevelsTable(levelsData);
        }
        if (constRes.status === 200) {
          constantsData = constRes.data.constants;
          renderConstantsTable(constantsData);
        }
        if (drillRes.status === 200) {
          drillsData = drillRes.data.drills;
          renderDrillSelect(drillsData);
        }
      }
    
      function renderLevelsTable(levels) {
        levelsTableTbody.innerHTML = '';
        levels.forEach(lvl => {
          const tr = document.createElement('tr');
          const totals = lvl.brick_totals || {};
          tr.innerHTML = `
            <td><strong>${lvl.level}</strong></td>
            <td>${lvl.name}</td>
            <td>${lvl.base_speed} / ${lvl.speed_cap}</td>
            <td><span 
style="display:inline-block;width:12px;height:12px;background:${lvl.accent};bord
er-radius:2px;vertical-align:middle;margin-right:4px;"></span>${lvl.accent}</td>
            <td>${totals.normal || 0} / ${totals.strong || 0} / ${totals.solid 
|| 0} / <strong>${totals.total || 0}</strong></td>
            <td><code 
style="font-size:0.7rem;word-break:break-all;">${lvl.digest}</code></td>
          `;
          levelsTableTbody.appendChild(tr);
        });
      }
    
      function renderConstantsTable(constants) {
        constantsTableTbody.innerHTML = '';
        constants.forEach(c => {
          const tr = document.createElement('tr');
          tr.innerHTML = `
            <td><code>${c.key}</code></td>
            <td><strong>${c.value}</strong></td>
          `;
          constantsTableTbody.appendChild(tr);
        });
      }
    
      function renderDrillSelect(drills) {
        selectDrill.innerHTML = '';
        drills.forEach(d => {
          const opt = document.createElement('option');
          opt.value = d.id;
          opt.textContent = d.name;
          selectDrill.appendChild(opt);
        });
        if (drills.length > 0) {
          drillDescText.textContent = drills[0].description;
        }
      }
    
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // Mechanics Lab Handlers
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      function loadSelectedDrill() {
        const drillId = selectDrill.value;
        const drillState = createDrillState(drillId);
        if (!drillState) return;
    
        labEngine = new GameEngine(drillState);
        labStepCounter = 0;
        isLabActive = true;
        updateLabTelemetry();
        addEvent(`Mechanics Lab: Loaded drill "${drillState.name}"`);
      }
    
      function advanceLabDrill() {
        if (!labEngine) loadSelectedDrill();
        const steps = labEngine.advanceSteps(120, true);
        labStepCounter += steps;
        updateLabTelemetry();
        addEvent(`Drill advanced ${steps} ticks (Total: ${labStepCounter})`);
      }
    
      function updateLabTelemetry() {
        if (!labEngine) return;
        tSteps.textContent = labStepCounter;
        tStatus.textContent = labEngine.status;
        tScore.textContent = labEngine.score.toLocaleString();
        tLives.textContent = labEngine.lives;
        tCombo.textContent = `x${labEngine.combo}`;
        tPaddleWidth.textContent = `${labEngine.paddle.width} px`;
        const sec = getWholeSeconds(labEngine.powerSeconds);
        tPower.textContent = labEngine.power ? `${labEngine.power.toUpperCase()}
(${sec}s)` : 'None';
    
        // Balls list
        tBallsCount.textContent = labEngine.balls.length;
        tBallsList.innerHTML = '';
        labEngine.balls.forEach(b => {
          const li = document.createElement('li');
          li.textContent = `${b.id}: pos=(${Math.round(b.x)}, 
${Math.round(b.y)}) vel=(${Math.round(b.vx)}, ${Math.round(b.vy)}) ${b.held ? 
'[HELD]' : ''} ${b.lost ? '[LOST]' : ''}`;
          tBallsList.appendChild(li);
        });
    
        // Drops list
        tDropsCount.textContent = labEngine.drops.length;
        tDropsList.innerHTML = '';
        labEngine.drops.forEach(d => {
          const li = document.createElement('li');
          li.textContent = `${d.type.toUpperCase()}: y=${Math.round(d.y)} 
(vy=${d.vy})`;
          tDropsList.appendChild(li);
        });
    
        // Bricks list
        const activeBricks = labEngine.bricks;
        tBricksCount.textContent = activeBricks.length;
        tBricksList.innerHTML = '';
        activeBricks.forEach(br => {
          const span = document.createElement('span');
          span.className = `brick-tag ${br.type} ${br.hp <= 0 ? 'broken' : ''}`;
          span.textContent = `${br.type[0].toUpperCase()}:${br.hp}`;
          tBricksList.appendChild(span);
        });
      }
    
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // Events Log
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      function addEvent(msg) {
        latestEventPill.textContent = msg;
        const li = document.createElement('li');
        const timeStr = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour12: false, hour:
'2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' });
        li.textContent = `[${timeStr}] ${msg}`;
        eventLogList.prepend(li);
    
        while (eventLogList.children.length > 30) {
          eventLogList.removeChild(eventLogList.lastChild);
        }
      }
    
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // UI & Canvas Renderer
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      function updateUI() {
        if (!engine) return;
    
        hudScore.textContent = engine.score.toLocaleString();
        hudLives.textContent = engine.lives;
    
        const currentLvlInfo = levelsData.find(l => l.level === engine.level);
        const lvlName = currentLvlInfo ? currentLvlInfo.name : '';
        hudLevel.textContent = `${engine.level} - ${lvlName}`;
    
        hudCombo.textContent = `x${engine.combo}`;
        const sec = getWholeSeconds(engine.powerSeconds);
        hudPower.textContent = engine.power ? `${engine.power.toUpperCase()} 
(${sec}s)` : 'None';
        hudStatus.textContent = engine.status.toUpperCase();
    
        // Accessible canvas label
        canvas.setAttribute('aria-label', `Brickfall Game Screen: Level 
${engine.level}, Score ${engine.score}, Lives ${engine.lives}, Status 
${engine.status}`);
    
        // Overlay display
        if (engine.status === 'paused') {
          overlayTitle.textContent = 'PAUSED';
          overlayDesc.textContent = 'Game is paused. Press P, Esc, or Resume to 
continue.';
          overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-primary" id="btn-overlay-resume">Resume [P]</button>';
          canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('btn-overlay-resume').onclick = () => {
            engine.status = 'playing';
            updateUI();
          };
        } else if (engine.status === 'ready') {
          overlayTitle.textContent = `LEVEL ${engine.level}: 
${lvlName.toUpperCase()}`;
          overlayDesc.textContent = 'Press Space or click Launch to serve the 
ball.';
          overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-primary" id="btn-overlay-launch">Launch Ball [Space]</button>';
          canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('btn-overlay-launch').onclick = () => {
            engine.launchBall();
            updateUI();
          };
        } else if (engine.status === 'life-lost') {
          overlayTitle.textContent = 'BALL LOST';
          overlayDesc.textContent = `Lives remaining: ${engine.lives}. Press 
Space to serve.`;
          overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-primary" id="btn-overlay-serve">Serve Ball [Space]</button>';
          canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('btn-overlay-serve').onclick = () => {
            engine.launchBall();
            updateUI();
          };
        } else if (engine.status === 'level-complete') {
          overlayTitle.textContent = 'LEVEL COMPLETE!';
          overlayDesc.textContent = `Cleared Level ${engine.level}! Bonus 
+${1000 * engine.level}. Total: ${engine.score.toLocaleString()}`;
          overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-primary" id="btn-overlay-continue">Next Level ▶</button>';
          canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('btn-overlay-continue').onclick = () => {
            progressLevel();
          };
        } else if (engine.status === 'game-over') {
          overlayTitle.textContent = 'GAME OVER';
          overlayDesc.textContent = `Final Score: 
${engine.score.toLocaleString()} at Level ${engine.level}. Press R or Restart.`;
          overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-danger" id="btn-overlay-restart">Restart Run [R]</button>';
          canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('btn-overlay-restart').onclick = () => {
            startNewGame(1);
          };
        } else if (engine.status === 'completed') {
          overlayTitle.textContent = 'VICTORY!';
          overlayDesc.textContent = `Congratulations! You cleared all 10 levels!
Final Score: ${engine.score.toLocaleString()}`;
          overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-primary" id="btn-overlay-restart-victory">Play Again [R]</button>';
          canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('btn-overlay-restart-victory').onclick = () =>
{
            startNewGame(1);
          };
        } else {
          canvasOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      }
    
      function renderGame(targetEngine) {
        if (!targetEngine) return;
    
        // Clear background
        ctx.fillStyle = '#06090e';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH, CONSTANTS.CANVAS_HEIGHT);
    
        // Subtle arcade grid lines
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(48, 54, 61, 0.25)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        for (let x = 0; x < CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH; x += 60) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(x, 0);
          ctx.lineTo(x, CONSTANTS.CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
        for (let y = 0; y < CONSTANTS.CANVAS_HEIGHT; y += 60) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(0, y);
          ctx.lineTo(CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH, y);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
    
        // Level Accent Color
        const lvlInfo = levelsData.find(l => l.level === targetEngine.level);
        const accentColor = lvlInfo ? lvlInfo.accent : '#58a6ff';
    
        // Render Bricks
        for (const brick of targetEngine.bricks) {
          if (brick.hp <= 0) continue;
    
          const bx = brick.x !== undefined ? brick.x : getBrickRect(brick.row, 
brick.column).x;
          const by = brick.y !== undefined ? brick.y : getBrickRect(brick.row, 
brick.column).y;
          const bw = brick.width || CONSTANTS.BRICK_WIDTH;
          const bh = brick.height || CONSTANTS.BRICK_HEIGHT;
    
          if (brick.type === 'normal') {
            ctx.fillStyle = accentColor;
            ctx.fillRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
            ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
            ctx.strokeRect(bx + 1, by + 1, bw - 2, bh - 2);
    
            // Brick label / diamond
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
            ctx.font = 'bold 9px monospace';
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
            ctx.fillText('100', bx + bw / 2, by + bh / 2);
          } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
            const isDamaged = brick.hp === 1;
            ctx.fillStyle = isDamaged ? '#d29922' : '#e3b341';
            ctx.fillRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
    
            // Double heavy border
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            ctx.strokeRect(bx + 1, by + 1, bw - 2, bh - 2);
    
            if (isDamaged) {
              // Visible damage crack lines
              ctx.strokeStyle = '#7a4e00';
              ctx.lineWidth = 2;
              ctx.beginPath();
              ctx.moveTo(bx + 8, by + 4);
              ctx.lineTo(bx + bw / 2, by + bh - 4);
              ctx.lineTo(bx + bw - 10, by + 6);
              ctx.stroke();
            }
    
            ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
            ctx.font = 'bold 9px monospace';
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
            ctx.fillText(isDamaged ? 'CRACK' : 'STRONG', bx + bw / 2, by + bh / 
2);
          } else if (brick.type === 'solid') {
            // Metallic slate with hazard stripes
            ctx.fillStyle = '#2d333b';
            ctx.fillRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
    
            // Diagonal hazard stripes
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#6e7681';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            for (let sx = -bh; sx < bw; sx += 12) {
              ctx.beginPath();
              ctx.moveTo(bx + sx, by + bh);
              ctx.lineTo(bx + sx + bh, by);
              ctx.stroke();
            }
    
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#adbac7';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            ctx.strokeRect(bx + 1, by + 1, bw - 2, bh - 2);
    
            ctx.fillStyle = '#f0f6fc';
            ctx.font = 'bold 8px monospace';
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
            ctx.fillText('SOLID', bx + bw / 2, by + bh / 2);
          }
        }
    
        // Render Drops
        for (const drop of targetEngine.drops) {
          ctx.save();
          let dropColor = '#58a6ff';
          let label = 'P';
          if (drop.type === 'wide') { dropColor = '#58a6ff'; label = 'WIDE'; }
          else if (drop.type === 'slow') { dropColor = '#3fb950'; label = 
'SLOW'; }
          else if (drop.type === 'multiball') { dropColor = '#bc8cff'; label = 
'MULTI'; }
          else if (drop.type === 'sticky') { dropColor = '#f778ba'; label = 
'STICK'; }
    
          ctx.fillStyle = dropColor;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.roundRect(drop.x, drop.y, drop.width, drop.height, 4);
          ctx.fill();
          ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
          ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
          ctx.stroke();
    
          ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
          ctx.font = 'bold 8px monospace';
          ctx.textAlign = 'center';
          ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
          ctx.fillText(label, drop.x + drop.width / 2, drop.y + drop.height / 
2);
          ctx.restore();
        }
    
        // Render Paddle
        const p = targetEngine.paddle;
        ctx.save();
        let paddleGradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(p.x, p.y, p.x, p.y + 
p.height);
        paddleGradient.addColorStop(0, '#58a6ff');
        paddleGradient.addColorStop(1, '#1f6feb');
    
        if (targetEngine.power === 'wide') {
          paddleGradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(p.x, p.y, p.x, p.y + 
p.height);
          paddleGradient.addColorStop(0, '#79c0ff');
          paddleGradient.addColorStop(1, '#388bfd');
        } else if (targetEngine.power === 'sticky') {
          paddleGradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(p.x, p.y, p.x, p.y + 
p.height);
          paddleGradient.addColorStop(0, '#f778ba');
          paddleGradient.addColorStop(1, '#bf4b8a');
        }
    
        ctx.fillStyle = paddleGradient;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.roundRect(p.x, p.y, p.width, p.height, 6);
        ctx.fill();
    
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.stroke();
    
        // Center guide line
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(p.x + p.width / 2, p.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(p.x + p.width / 2, p.y + p.height - 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
    
        // Render Balls
        for (const ball of targetEngine.balls) {
          if (ball.lost) continue;
    
          ctx.save();
          ctx.shadowColor = '#58a6ff';
          ctx.shadowBlur = 8;
          ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
    
          ctx.strokeStyle = '#58a6ff';
          ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
          ctx.stroke();
          ctx.restore();
        }
      }
    
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // Input Handling & Controls
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      function handlePaddleMove(deltaX) {
        if (!engine) return;
        assistEnabled = false; // Turn off assist on manual input
        updateAssistButton();
    
        engine.paddle.vx = deltaX * 60; // Approximate velocity
        engine.paddle.x += deltaX;
        engine.paddle.x = clamp(engine.paddle.x, 0, CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH - 
engine.paddle.width);
      }
    
      function updateAssistButton() {
        btnAssist.setAttribute('aria-checked', assistEnabled ? 'true' : 
'false');
        assistStateText.textContent = assistEnabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF';
      }
    
      function applyAssistAI(targetEngine, dt) {
        if (!assistEnabled || !targetEngine || targetEngine.status !== 
'playing') return;
    
        // Find the most urgent threat (ball falling towards paddle or closest 
reachable item)
        const activeBalls = targetEngine.balls.filter(b => !b.lost && !b.held &&
b.vy > 0);
        let targetX = targetEngine.paddle.x + targetEngine.paddle.width / 2;
    
        if (activeBalls.length > 0) {
          // Pick lowest ball
          const lowestBall = activeBalls.reduce((prev, curr) => curr.y > prev.y 
? curr : prev, activeBalls[0]);
          targetX = lowestBall.x;
        } else if (targetEngine.drops.length > 0) {
          const lowestDrop = targetEngine.drops.reduce((prev, curr) => curr.y > 
prev.y ? curr : prev, targetEngine.drops[0]);
          targetX = lowestDrop.x + lowestDrop.width / 2;
        }
    
        const paddleCenter = targetEngine.paddle.x + targetEngine.paddle.width /
2;
        const diff = targetX - paddleCenter;
        const maxSpeed = 700; // Ordinary control speed
        const stepDist = maxSpeed * dt;
    
        if (Math.abs(diff) > 2) {
          const move = Math.sign(diff) * Math.min(Math.abs(diff), stepDist);
          targetEngine.paddle.vx = move / dt;
          targetEngine.paddle.x += move;
          targetEngine.paddle.x = clamp(targetEngine.paddle.x, 0, 
CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH - targetEngine.paddle.width);
        } else {
          targetEngine.paddle.vx = 0;
        }
      }
    
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // Main Animation Loop
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      function gameLoop(currentTime) {
        const elapsed = (currentTime - lastFrameTime) / 1000;
        lastFrameTime = currentTime;
    
        const dt = CONSTANTS.DT;
        const currentActiveEngine = isLabActive ? labEngine : engine;
    
        if (currentActiveEngine) {
          // Manual Keyboard Paddle Movement
          if (keys.left) handlePaddleMove(-450 * elapsed);
          if (keys.right) handlePaddleMove(450 * elapsed);
    
          // Assist AI
          applyAssistAI(currentActiveEngine, elapsed);
    
          // Fixed-timestep simulation steps during 'playing' state
          if (currentActiveEngine.status === 'playing') {
            accumulator += Math.min(elapsed, 0.1);
            while (accumulator >= dt) {
              const prevStatus = currentActiveEngine.status;
              currentActiveEngine.step(dt);
              accumulator -= dt;
    
              if (currentActiveEngine.status !== prevStatus) {
                // State transition occurred
                updateUI();
                if (currentActiveEngine.status === 'game-over') {
                  finishRun('game-over');
                } else if (currentActiveEngine.status === 'completed') {
                  finishRun('completed');
                }
                break;
              }
            }
          } else {
            accumulator = 0;
          }
    
          // Render
          renderGame(currentActiveEngine);
        }
    
        requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop);
      }
    
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // Event Listeners Setup
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      function setupEvents() {
        // Demo login buttons
        document.querySelectorAll('.btn-demo').forEach(btn => {
          btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const email = btn.getAttribute('data-email');
            loginEmail.value = email;
            loginPassword.value = 'password123';
            handleLogin(email, 'password123');
          });
        });
    
        // Login Form
        loginForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          handleLogin(loginEmail.value, loginPassword.value);
        });
    
        // Logout
        btnLogout.addEventListener('click', handleSignOut);
    
        // Keyboard Shortcuts
        window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
          if (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT' || e.target.tagName === 'SELECT') 
return;
    
          if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') {
            keys.left = true;
          } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd' || e.key === 'D') {
            keys.right = true;
          } else if (e.key === ' ' || e.code === 'Space') {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (engine) {
              if (engine.status === 'ready' || engine.status === 'life-lost') {
                engine.launchBall();
                updateUI();
              } else if (engine.status === 'playing') {
                // Release sticky ball if any
                engine.launchBall();
              }
            }
          } else if (e.key === 'p' || e.key === 'P' || e.key === 'Escape') {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (engine) {
              if (engine.status === 'playing') {
                engine.status = 'paused';
                saveActiveRun();
                updateUI();
              } else if (engine.status === 'paused') {
                engine.status = 'playing';
                updateUI();
              }
            }
          } else if (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R') {
            if (engine && (engine.status === 'game-over' || engine.status === 
'completed')) {
              e.preventDefault();
              startNewGame(1);
            }
          }
        });
    
        window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
          if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') keys.left
= false;
          if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd' || e.key === 'D') 
keys.right = false;
        });
    
        // Pointer / Mouse Canvas Movement
        canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
          if (!engine) return;
          assistEnabled = false;
          updateAssistButton();
    
          const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
          const scaleX = CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH / rect.width;
          const mouseX = (e.clientX - rect.left) * scaleX;
          engine.paddle.x = clamp(mouseX - engine.paddle.width / 2, 0, 
CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH - engine.paddle.width);
        });
    
        // Toolbar Buttons
        btnLaunch.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (engine) {
            engine.launchBall();
            updateUI();
          }
        });
    
        btnPause.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (!engine) return;
          if (engine.status === 'playing') {
            engine.status = 'paused';
            saveActiveRun();
          } else if (engine.status === 'paused') {
            engine.status = 'playing';
          }
          updateUI();
        });
    
        btnRestart.addEventListener('click', () => {
          startNewGame(1);
        });
    
        btnAssist.addEventListener('click', () => {
          assistEnabled = !assistEnabled;
          updateAssistButton();
          addEvent(`Assist Paddle: ${assistEnabled ? 'ENABLED' : 'DISABLED'}`);
        });
    
        btnNewRun.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const chosen = Number(selectLevel.value) || 1;
          startNewGame(chosen);
        });
    
        // Touch controls
        touchLeft.addEventListener('pointerdown', () => { keys.left = true; });
        touchLeft.addEventListener('pointerup', () => { keys.left = false; });
        touchRight.addEventListener('pointerdown', () => { keys.right = true; 
});
        touchRight.addEventListener('pointerup', () => { keys.right = false; });
        touchLaunch.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (engine) {
            engine.launchBall();
            updateUI();
          }
        });
    
        // Refresh Buttons
        btnRefreshLb.addEventListener('click', loadLeaderboard);
        btnRefreshRuns.addEventListener('click', loadPersonalRuns);
    
        // Mechanics Lab Modal Toggle
        btnLabToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
          labModal.classList.remove('hidden');
          loadSelectedDrill();
        });
    
        btnCloseLab.addEventListener('click', () => {
          labModal.classList.add('hidden');
          isLabActive = false;
        });
    
        // Snapshot Modal Close
        btnCloseSnapshot.addEventListener('click', () => {
          snapshotModal.classList.add('hidden');
        });
    
        // Lab Tabs
        tabDrillsBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          tabDrillsBtn.classList.add('active');
          tabLevelsBtn.classList.remove('active');
          tabConstantsBtn.classList.remove('active');
          tabDrills.classList.remove('hidden');
          tabLevels.classList.add('hidden');
          tabConstants.classList.add('hidden');
        });
    
        tabLevelsBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          tabLevelsBtn.classList.add('active');
          tabDrillsBtn.classList.remove('active');
          tabConstantsBtn.classList.remove('active');
          tabLevels.classList.remove('hidden');
          tabDrills.classList.add('hidden');
          tabConstants.classList.add('hidden');
        });
    
        tabConstantsBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          tabConstantsBtn.classList.add('active');
          tabDrillsBtn.classList.remove('active');
          tabLevelsBtn.classList.remove('active');
          tabConstants.classList.remove('hidden');
          tabDrills.classList.add('hidden');
          tabLevels.classList.add('hidden');
        });
    
        selectDrill.addEventListener('change', () => {
          const selected = drillsData.find(d => d.id === selectDrill.value);
          if (selected) drillDescText.textContent = selected.description;
        });
    
        btnLoadDrill.addEventListener('click', loadSelectedDrill);
        btnAdvanceDrill.addEventListener('click', advanceLabDrill);
        btnDrillLaunch.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (labEngine) {
            labEngine.launchBall();
            updateLabTelemetry();
          }
        });
    
        // Periodic throttled save
        setInterval(() => {
          if (engine && engine.status === 'playing') {
            saveActiveRun();
          }
        }, 5000);
      }
    
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      // Initialization
      // -------------------------------------------------------------
      setupEvents();
      checkExistingSession();
      requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop);
    
    })();

+120|    const headers = Object.assign({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
options.headers || {});
+121|    if (authToken) {
+122|      headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' + authToken;
+123|    }

+127|

+129|      // Discard token and show sign in

+132|    }

+250|  async function startNewGame(levelNum) {

+277|    } else {
+278|      setSyncStatus('conflict', res.data.error);
+279|    }
+280|  }
+281|
+282|  async function saveActiveRun() {
+283|    if (!engine || isMutating || engine.isMechanicsLab) return;
+284|    if (engine.status === 'game-over' || engine.status === 'completed') 
return;
+285|
+286|    const opId = generateOpId();
+287|    const snapshot = engine.getSnapshot();
+288|
+289|    const res = await apiFetch('/api/game/save', {
+290|      method: 'POST',
+291|      body: JSON.stringify({
+292|        expected_revision: currentUser.revision,
+293|        operation_id: opId,
+294|        run_id: activeRun ? activeRun.run_id : 'active',
+295|        state: snapshot
+296|      })
+297|    });
+298|
+299|    if (res.status === 200) {
+300|      currentUser.revision = res.data.revision;
+301|      revTag.textContent = `Rev #${currentUser.revision}`;
+302|      setSyncStatus('synced');
+303|    } else if (res.status === 409) {
+304|      handleConflict(res.data);
+305|    }
+306|  }
+307|
+308|  async function progressLevel() {
+309|    if (isMutating) return;
+310|    isMutating = true;
+311|    setSyncStatus('syncing');
+312|
+313|    const completedLvl = engine.level;
+314|    const currentScore = engine.score;
+315|    const opId = generateOpId();
+316|
+317|    const res = await apiFetch('/api/game/progress', {
+318|      method: 'POST',
+319|      body: JSON.stringify({
+320|        expected_revision: currentUser.revision,
+321|        operation_id: opId,
+322|        run_id: activeRun ? activeRun.run_id : 'active',
+323|        completed_level: completedLvl,
+324|        score: currentScore,
+325|        next_level: Math.min(10, completedLvl + 1),
+326|        state: engine.getSnapshot()
+327|      })
+328|    });
+329|
+330|    isMutating = false;
+331|
+332|    if (res.status === 200) {
+333|      currentUser.revision = res.data.revision;
+334|      currentUser.highest_level = res.data.highest_level;
+335|      currentUser.best_score = res.data.best_score;
+336|      revTag.textContent = `Rev #${currentUser.revision}`;
+337|      setSyncStatus('synced');
+338|      updateLevelSelect();
+339|      loadLeaderboard();
+340|
+341|      if (completedLvl < 10) {
+342|        startNewGame(completedLvl + 1);
+343|      }
+344|    } else if (res.status === 409) {
+345|      handleConflict(res.data);
+346|    }
+347|  }
+348|
+349|  async function finishRun(outcome) {
+350|    if (isMutating) return;
+351|    isMutating = true;
+352|    setSyncStatus('syncing');
+353|
+354|    const opId = generateOpId();
+355|    const finalScore = engine.score;
+356|    const finalLevel = engine.level;
+357|    const snapshot = engine.getSnapshot();
+358|
+359|    const res = await apiFetch('/api/game/finish', {
+360|      method: 'POST',
+361|      body: JSON.stringify({
+362|        expected_revision: currentUser.revision,
+363|        operation_id: opId,
+364|        run_id: activeRun ? activeRun.run_id : `run_${Date.now()}`,
+365|        outcome: outcome,
+366|        level: finalLevel,
+367|        score: finalScore,
+368|        snapshot: snapshot
+369|      })
+370|    });
+371|
+372|    isMutating = false;
+373|
+374|    if (res.status === 200) {
+375|      currentUser.revision = res.data.revision;
+376|      currentUser.best_score = res.data.best_score;
+377|      revTag.textContent = `Rev #${currentUser.revision}`;
+378|      activeRun = null;
+379|      setSyncStatus('synced');
+380|      renderLeaderboardTable(res.data.leaderboard);
+381|      loadPersonalRuns();
+382|    } else if (res.status === 409) {
+383|      handleConflict(res.data);
+384|    }
+385|  }
+386|
+387|  function handleConflict(conflictData) {
+388|    currentUser.revision = conflictData.current_revision;
+389|    revTag.textContent = `Rev #${currentUser.revision}`;
+390|
+391|    if (conflictData.active_run) {
+392|      activeRun = conflictData.active_run;
+393|      engine = new GameEngine(activeRun);
+394|      engine.status = 'paused';
+395|    }
+396|
+397|    setSyncStatus('conflict', `Sync conflict: Server revision 
${conflictData.current_revision} won. Game paused and reconciled.`);
+398|    addEvent(`Conflict reconciled: Server revision 
#${conflictData.current_revision}`);
+399|    updateUI();
+400|  }
+401|

Date(r.achieved_at).toLocaleDateString() : 'N/A';

style="font-size:0.65rem;padding:2px 4px;">${r.initials}</span></td>

+425|    });

btn-inspect-run" data-runid="${r.run_id}">Inspect</button></td>
+450|      `;

+467|  async function loadLevelsAndConstants() {
+468|    const [lvlRes, constRes, drillRes] = await Promise.all([
+469|      apiFetch('/api/levels'),
+470|      apiFetch('/api/constants'),
+471|      apiFetch('/api/drills')
+472|    ]);
+473|
+474|    if (lvlRes.status === 200) {
+475|      levelsData = lvlRes.data.levels;
+476|      renderLevelsTable(levelsData);
+477|    }
+478|    if (constRes.status === 200) {
+479|      constantsData = constRes.data.constants;
+480|      renderConstantsTable(constantsData);
+481|    }
+482|    if (drillRes.status === 200) {
+483|      drillsData = drillRes.data.drills;
+484|      renderDrillSelect(drillsData);
+485|    }
+486|  }

+488|  function renderLevelsTable(levels) {

+497|        <td><span 
style="display:inline-block;width:12px;height:12px;background:${lvl.accent};bord
er-radius:2px;vertical-align:middle;margin-right:4px;"></span>${lvl.accent}</td>
+498|        <td>${totals.normal || 0} / ${totals.strong || 0} / ${totals.solid 
|| 0} / <strong>${totals.total || 0}</strong></td>
+499|        <td><code 
style="font-size:0.7rem;word-break:break-all;">${lvl.digest}</code></td>
+500|      `;
+501|      levelsTableTbody.appendChild(tr);
+502|    });
+503|  }

+530|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+531|  // Mechanics Lab Handlers
+532|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+533|  function loadSelectedDrill() {
+534|    const drillId = selectDrill.value;
+535|    const drillState = createDrillState(drillId);
+536|    if (!drillState) return;
+537|
+538|    labEngine = new GameEngine(drillState);
+539|    labStepCounter = 0;
+540|    isLabActive = true;
+541|    updateLabTelemetry();
+542|    addEvent(`Mechanics Lab: Loaded drill "${drillState.name}"`);
+543|  }
+544|
+545|  function advanceLabDrill() {
+546|    if (!labEngine) loadSelectedDrill();
+547|    const steps = labEngine.advanceSteps(120, true);
+548|    labStepCounter += steps;
+549|    updateLabTelemetry();
+550|    addEvent(`Drill advanced ${steps} ticks (Total: ${labStepCounter})`);
+551|  }
+552|
+553|  function updateLabTelemetry() {
+554|    if (!labEngine) return;
+555|    tSteps.textContent = labStepCounter;
+556|    tStatus.textContent = labEngine.status;
+557|    tScore.textContent = labEngine.score.toLocaleString();
+558|    tLives.textContent = labEngine.lives;
+559|    tCombo.textContent = `x${labEngine.combo}`;
+560|    tPaddleWidth.textContent = `${labEngine.paddle.width} px`;
+561|    const sec = getWholeSeconds(labEngine.powerSeconds);
+562|    tPower.textContent = labEngine.power ? 
`${labEngine.power.toUpperCase()} (${sec}s)` : 'None';
+563|
+564|    // Balls list
+565|    tBallsCount.textContent = labEngine.balls.length;
+566|    tBallsList.innerHTML = '';
+567|    labEngine.balls.forEach(b => {
+568|      const li = document.createElement('li');
+569|      li.textContent = `${b.id}: pos=(${Math.round(b.x)}, 
${Math.round(b.y)}) vel=(${Math.round(b.vx)}, ${Math.round(b.vy)}) ${b.held ? 
'[HELD]' : ''} ${b.lost ? '[LOST]' : ''}`;
+570|      tBallsList.appendChild(li);
+571|    });
+572|
+573|    // Drops list
+574|    tDropsCount.textContent = labEngine.drops.length;
+575|    tDropsList.innerHTML = '';
+576|    labEngine.drops.forEach(d => {
+577|      const li = document.createElement('li');
+578|      li.textContent = `${d.type.toUpperCase()}: y=${Math.round(d.y)} 
(vy=${d.vy})`;
+579|      tDropsList.appendChild(li);
+580|    });
+581|
+582|    // Bricks list
+583|    const activeBricks = labEngine.bricks;
+584|    tBricksCount.textContent = activeBricks.length;
+585|    tBricksList.innerHTML = '';
+586|    activeBricks.forEach(br => {
+587|      const span = document.createElement('span');
+588|      span.className = `brick-tag ${br.type} ${br.hp <= 0 ? 'broken' : 
''}`;
+589|      span.textContent = `${br.type[0].toUpperCase()}:${br.hp}`;
+590|      tBricksList.appendChild(span);
+591|    });
+592|  }

+594|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+595|  // Events Log
+596|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+597|  function addEvent(msg) {
+598|    latestEventPill.textContent = msg;
+599|    const li = document.createElement('li');
+600|    const timeStr = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour12: false, 
hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' });
+601|    li.textContent = `[${timeStr}] ${msg}`;
+602|    eventLogList.prepend(li);
+603|
+604|    while (eventLogList.children.length > 30) {
+605|      eventLogList.removeChild(eventLogList.lastChild);
+606|    }
+607|  }
+608|
+609|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+610|  // UI & Canvas Renderer
+611|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+612|  function updateUI() {
+613|    if (!engine) return;
+614|
+615|    hudScore.textContent = engine.score.toLocaleString();
+616|    hudLives.textContent = engine.lives;
+617|
+618|    const currentLvlInfo = levelsData.find(l => l.level === engine.level);
+619|    const lvlName = currentLvlInfo ? currentLvlInfo.name : '';
+620|    hudLevel.textContent = `${engine.level} - ${lvlName}`;
+621|
+622|    hudCombo.textContent = `x${engine.combo}`;
+623|    const sec = getWholeSeconds(engine.powerSeconds);
+624|    hudPower.textContent = engine.power ? `${engine.power.toUpperCase()} 
(${sec}s)` : 'None';
+625|    hudStatus.textContent = engine.status.toUpperCase();
+626|
+627|    // Accessible canvas label
+628|    canvas.setAttribute('aria-label', `Brickfall Game Screen: Level 
${engine.level}, Score ${engine.score}, Lives ${engine.lives}, Status 
${engine.status}`);
+629|
+630|    // Overlay display
+631|    if (engine.status === 'paused') {
+632|      overlayTitle.textContent = 'PAUSED';
+633|      overlayDesc.textContent = 'Game is paused. Press P, Esc, or Resume to
continue.';
+634|      overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-primary" id="btn-overlay-resume">Resume [P]</button>';
+635|      canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
+636|      document.getElementById('btn-overlay-resume').onclick = () => {
+637|        engine.status = 'playing';
+638|        updateUI();
+639|      };
+640|    } else if (engine.status === 'ready') {
+641|      overlayTitle.textContent = `LEVEL ${engine.level}: 
${lvlName.toUpperCase()}`;
+642|      overlayDesc.textContent = 'Press Space or click Launch to serve the 
ball.';
+643|      overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-primary" id="btn-overlay-launch">Launch Ball [Space]</button>';
+644|      canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
+645|      document.getElementById('btn-overlay-launch').onclick = () => {
+646|        engine.launchBall();
+647|        updateUI();
+648|      };
+649|    } else if (engine.status === 'life-lost') {
+650|      overlayTitle.textContent = 'BALL LOST';
+651|      overlayDesc.textContent = `Lives remaining: ${engine.lives}. Press 
Space to serve.`;
+652|      overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-primary" id="btn-overlay-serve">Serve Ball [Space]</button>';
+653|      canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
+654|      document.getElementById('btn-overlay-serve').onclick = () => {
+655|        engine.launchBall();
+656|        updateUI();
+657|      };
+658|    } else if (engine.status === 'level-complete') {
+659|      overlayTitle.textContent = 'LEVEL COMPLETE!';
+660|      overlayDesc.textContent = `Cleared Level ${engine.level}! Bonus 
+${1000 * engine.level}. Total: ${engine.score.toLocaleString()}`;
+661|      overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-primary" id="btn-overlay-continue">Next Level ▶</button>';
+662|      canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
+663|      document.getElementById('btn-overlay-continue').onclick = () => {
+664|        progressLevel();
+665|      };
+666|    } else if (engine.status === 'game-over') {
+667|      overlayTitle.textContent = 'GAME OVER';
+668|      overlayDesc.textContent = `Final Score: 
${engine.score.toLocaleString()} at Level ${engine.level}. Press R or Restart.`;
+669|      overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-danger" id="btn-overlay-restart">Restart Run [R]</button>';
+670|      canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
+671|      document.getElementById('btn-overlay-restart').onclick = () => {
+672|        startNewGame(1);
+673|      };
+674|    } else if (engine.status === 'completed') {
+675|      overlayTitle.textContent = 'VICTORY!';
+676|      overlayDesc.textContent = `Congratulations! You cleared all 10 
levels! Final Score: ${engine.score.toLocaleString()}`;
+677|      overlayButtons.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="btn 
btn-primary" id="btn-overlay-restart-victory">Play Again [R]</button>';
+678|      canvasOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
+679|      document.getElementById('btn-overlay-restart-victory').onclick = () 
=> {
+680|        startNewGame(1);
+681|      };
+682|    } else {
+683|      canvasOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
+684|    }
+685|  }
+686|
+687|  function renderGame(targetEngine) {
+688|    if (!targetEngine) return;
+689|
+690|    // Clear background
+691|    ctx.fillStyle = '#06090e';
+692|    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH, CONSTANTS.CANVAS_HEIGHT);
+693|
+694|    // Subtle arcade grid lines
+695|    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(48, 54, 61, 0.25)';
+696|    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
+697|    for (let x = 0; x < CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH; x += 60) {
+698|      ctx.beginPath();
+699|      ctx.moveTo(x, 0);
+700|      ctx.lineTo(x, CONSTANTS.CANVAS_HEIGHT);
+701|      ctx.stroke();
+702|    }
+703|    for (let y = 0; y < CONSTANTS.CANVAS_HEIGHT; y += 60) {
+704|      ctx.beginPath();
+705|      ctx.moveTo(0, y);
+706|      ctx.lineTo(CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH, y);
+707|      ctx.stroke();
+708|    }
+709|
+710|    // Level Accent Color
+711|    const lvlInfo = levelsData.find(l => l.level === targetEngine.level);
+712|    const accentColor = lvlInfo ? lvlInfo.accent : '#58a6ff';
+713|
+714|    // Render Bricks
+715|    for (const brick of targetEngine.bricks) {
+716|      if (brick.hp <= 0) continue;
+717|
+718|      const bx = brick.x !== undefined ? brick.x : getBrickRect(brick.row, 
brick.column).x;
+719|      const by = brick.y !== undefined ? brick.y : getBrickRect(brick.row, 
brick.column).y;
+720|      const bw = brick.width || CONSTANTS.BRICK_WIDTH;
+721|      const bh = brick.height || CONSTANTS.BRICK_HEIGHT;
+722|
+723|      if (brick.type === 'normal') {
+724|        ctx.fillStyle = accentColor;
+725|        ctx.fillRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
+726|        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
+727|        ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
+728|        ctx.strokeRect(bx + 1, by + 1, bw - 2, bh - 2);
+729|
+730|        // Brick label / diamond
+731|        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
+732|        ctx.font = 'bold 9px monospace';
+733|        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
+734|        ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
+735|        ctx.fillText('100', bx + bw / 2, by + bh / 2);
+736|      } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
+737|        const isDamaged = brick.hp === 1;
+738|        ctx.fillStyle = isDamaged ? '#d29922' : '#e3b341';
+739|        ctx.fillRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
+740|
+741|        // Double heavy border
+742|        ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
+743|        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
+744|        ctx.strokeRect(bx + 1, by + 1, bw - 2, bh - 2);
+745|
+746|        if (isDamaged) {
+747|          // Visible damage crack lines
+748|          ctx.strokeStyle = '#7a4e00';
+749|          ctx.lineWidth = 2;
+750|          ctx.beginPath();
+751|          ctx.moveTo(bx + 8, by + 4);
+752|          ctx.lineTo(bx + bw / 2, by + bh - 4);
+753|          ctx.lineTo(bx + bw - 10, by + 6);
+754|          ctx.stroke();
+755|        }
+756|
+757|        ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
+758|        ctx.font = 'bold 9px monospace';
+759|        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
+760|        ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
+761|        ctx.fillText(isDamaged ? 'CRACK' : 'STRONG', bx + bw / 2, by + bh /
2);
+762|      } else if (brick.type === 'solid') {
+763|        // Metallic slate with hazard stripes
+764|        ctx.fillStyle = '#2d333b';
+765|        ctx.fillRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
+766|
+767|        // Diagonal hazard stripes
+768|        ctx.strokeStyle = '#6e7681';
+769|        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
+770|        for (let sx = -bh; sx < bw; sx += 12) {
+771|          ctx.beginPath();
+772|          ctx.moveTo(bx + sx, by + bh);
+773|          ctx.lineTo(bx + sx + bh, by);
+774|          ctx.stroke();
+775|        }
+776|
+777|        ctx.strokeStyle = '#adbac7';
+778|        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
+779|        ctx.strokeRect(bx + 1, by + 1, bw - 2, bh - 2);
+780|
+781|        ctx.fillStyle = '#f0f6fc';
+782|        ctx.font = 'bold 8px monospace';
+783|        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
+784|        ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
+785|        ctx.fillText('SOLID', bx + bw / 2, by + bh / 2);
+786|      }
+787|    }
+788|
+789|    // Render Drops
+790|    for (const drop of targetEngine.drops) {
+791|      ctx.save();
+792|      let dropColor = '#58a6ff';
+793|      let label = 'P';
+794|      if (drop.type === 'wide') { dropColor = '#58a6ff'; label = 'WIDE'; }

'STICK'; }
+798|
+799|      ctx.fillStyle = dropColor;
+800|      ctx.beginPath();
+801|      ctx.roundRect(drop.x, drop.y, drop.width, drop.height, 4);
+802|      ctx.fill();
+803|      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
+804|      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
+805|      ctx.stroke();
+806|

+810|      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
+811|      ctx.fillText(label, drop.x + drop.width / 2, drop.y + drop.height /

+812|      ctx.restore();

+815|    // Render Paddle
+816|    const p = targetEngine.paddle;
+817|    ctx.save();

+819|    paddleGradient.addColorStop(0, '#58a6ff');

+839|    ctx.stroke();

+853|

+857|      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';

+860|      ctx.fill();

+869|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+870|  // Input Handling & Controls
+871|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+872|  function handlePaddleMove(deltaX) {
+873|    if (!engine) return;
+874|    assistEnabled = false; // Turn off assist on manual input
+875|    updateAssistButton();
+876|
+877|    engine.paddle.vx = deltaX * 60; // Approximate velocity
+878|    engine.paddle.x += deltaX;
+879|    engine.paddle.x = clamp(engine.paddle.x, 0, CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH - 
engine.paddle.width);
+880|  }
+881|
+882|  function updateAssistButton() {
+883|    btnAssist.setAttribute('aria-checked', assistEnabled ? 'true' : 
'false');
+884|    assistStateText.textContent = assistEnabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF';
+885|  }
+886|
+887|  function applyAssistAI(targetEngine, dt) {
+888|    if (!assistEnabled || !targetEngine || targetEngine.status !== 
'playing') return;
+889|
+890|    // Find the most urgent threat (ball falling towards paddle or closest 
reachable item)
+891|    const activeBalls = targetEngine.balls.filter(b => !b.lost && !b.held 
&& b.vy > 0);
+892|    let targetX = targetEngine.paddle.x + targetEngine.paddle.width / 2;
+893|
+894|    if (activeBalls.length > 0) {
+895|      // Pick lowest ball
+896|      const lowestBall = activeBalls.reduce((prev, curr) => curr.y > prev.y
? curr : prev, activeBalls[0]);
+897|      targetX = lowestBall.x;
+898|    } else if (targetEngine.drops.length > 0) {
+899|      const lowestDrop = targetEngine.drops.reduce((prev, curr) => curr.y >
prev.y ? curr : prev, targetEngine.drops[0]);
+900|      targetX = lowestDrop.x + lowestDrop.width / 2;
+901|    }
+902|
+903|    const paddleCenter = targetEngine.paddle.x + targetEngine.paddle.width 
/ 2;
+904|    const diff = targetX - paddleCenter;
+905|    const maxSpeed = 700; // Ordinary control speed
+906|    const stepDist = maxSpeed * dt;
+907|
+908|    if (Math.abs(diff) > 2) {
+909|      const move = Math.sign(diff) * Math.min(Math.abs(diff), stepDist);
+910|      targetEngine.paddle.vx = move / dt;
+911|      targetEngine.paddle.x += move;
+912|      targetEngine.paddle.x = clamp(targetEngine.paddle.x, 0, 
CONSTANTS.CANVAS_WIDTH - targetEngine.paddle.width);
+913|    } else {
+914|      targetEngine.paddle.vx = 0;
+915|    }
+916|  }
+917|
+918|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+919|  // Main Animation Loop
+920|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+921|  function gameLoop(currentTime) {
+922|    const elapsed = (currentTime - lastFrameTime) / 1000;
+923|    lastFrameTime = currentTime;
+924|
+925|    const dt = CONSTANTS.DT;
+926|    const currentActiveEngine = isLabActive ? labEngine : engine;
+927|
+928|    if (currentActiveEngine) {
+929|      // Manual Keyboard Paddle Movement
+930|      if (keys.left) handlePaddleMove(-450 * elapsed);
+931|      if (keys.right) handlePaddleMove(450 * elapsed);
+932|
+933|      // Assist AI
+934|      applyAssistAI(currentActiveEngine, elapsed);
+935|
+936|      // Fixed-timestep simulation steps during 'playing' state
+937|      if (currentActiveEngine.status === 'playing') {
+938|        accumulator += Math.min(elapsed, 0.1);
+939|        while (accumulator >= dt) {
+940|          const prevStatus = currentActiveEngine.status;
+941|          currentActiveEngine.step(dt);
+942|          accumulator -= dt;
+943|
+944|          if (currentActiveEngine.status !== prevStatus) {
+945|            // State transition occurred
+946|            updateUI();
+947|            if (currentActiveEngine.status === 'game-over') {
+948|              finishRun('game-over');
+949|            } else if (currentActiveEngine.status === 'completed') {
+950|              finishRun('completed');
+951|            }
+952|            break;
+953|          }
+954|        }
+955|      } else {
+956|        accumulator = 0;
+957|      }
+958|
+959|      // Render
+960|      renderGame(currentActiveEngine);
+961|    }
+962|
+963|    requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop);
+964|  }

Summary: Attach physics exports to window in browser

Reasoning:
**Ensuring Cross-Scope Accessibility in JavaScript Modules**

Alright, let's think this through. I need to make absolutely sure that 
`getWholeSeconds`, specifically, and other functions and objects from my 
`physics.js` and `drills.js` files are universally accessible when these scripts
are loaded in a browser environment. My primary concern here is how these 
components are exposed when the scripts are loaded using the `<script>` tag. Are
they automatically added to the `window` object?

Let's quickly check `physics.js`. Ah, yes, there's a conditional `if (typeof 
module !== 'undefined' && module.exports)` block there. That suggests this file 
is designed with CommonJS modules in mind. So, when loaded via `<script>`, the 
functions and objects defined within won't automatically be global. This is 
probably also the case with `drills.js` if it's structured similarly.

Therefore, `window.GameEngine`, `window.CONSTANTS`, `window.getBrickRect`, and 
even `window.getWholeSeconds`, need to be explicitly attached to `window` or 
`globalThis`, to make sure they are accessible everywhere, particularly in 
strict mode or when script tags are loaded in different order.

Let's go look at how these functions and classes are defined. Okay, in 
`physics.js` I see a `class GameEngine`, `const CONSTANTS`, and regular 
functions like `getBrickRect` and the one I'm concerned about - 
`getWholeSeconds`. Similarly, in `drills.js`, I'll find its functions and 
objects.

So, if these scripts are loaded through a `<script>` tag, the `class 
GameEngine`, `const CONSTANTS`, and these functions are, by default, in the 
global script scope, but in strict mode, or across separate script tags, this 
might not work. Attaching them to `window` or `globalThis` will ensure they are 
accessible everywhere.

I'll need to modify both `physics.js` and `drills.js` to include a check for the
existence of `window` (or `globalThis` for broader compatibility). If `window` 
is defined, I'll explicitly attach each key function, class, and constant to it.
I'll add something like this in `physics.js`:

```javascript
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  window.CONSTANTS = CONSTANTS;
  window.getBrickRect = getBrickRect;
  window.GameEngine = GameEngine;
  window.getWholeSeconds = getWholeSeconds;
  window.clamp = clamp;
}
```

And in `drills.js` it'll look like:

```javascript
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  window.createDrillState = createDrillState;
  window.createMiraCheckpoint = createMiraCheckpoint;
  window.createDevCheckpoint = createDevCheckpoint;
  window.DRILL_SUMMARIES = DRILL_SUMMARIES;
}
```

That should do it. Now, the functions and constants will be available in the 
global scope when the scripts are loaded in the browser, regardless of how 
they're loaded or what environment is used. This is good practice.

Here's my summary, as if I were thinking these thoughts:

**Adding Robustness to the Drills Module**

Okay, so I'm revisiting the `drills.js` module. I need to make sure this code is
production-ready and as robust as possible. That means thinking about where this

to use it in a Node.js context, or a server-side rendering setup? We need to 
prevent potential errors.

Therefore, it makes perfect sense to wrap the export block with `if (typeof 
window !== 'undefined')`. This will ensure that the code within that block, 
which likely relies on the `window` object (and therefore a browser context), is
only executed and exported when a window is defined. This is a common pattern to
ensure that the code is safe to import on the server-side, preventing a crash. 
This guards against those scenarios, making the module much more flexible and 
resilient. It's a simple change, but it adds a significant level of protection.
