$ rm -f /app/brickfall.db* && node -e "
const http = require('http');
const app = require('/app/server.js');

const server = app.listen(3000, async () => {
  console.log('Server started on port 3000');

  async function get(path, headers = {}) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      http.get({ hostname: '127.0.0.1', port: 3000, path, headers }, res => {
        let data = '';
        res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
        res.on('end', () => resolve({ status: res.statusCode, headers: 
res.headers, body: data }));
      }).on('error', reject);
    });
  }

  async function post(path, body, headers = {}) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const payload = JSON.stringify(body);
      const req = http.request({
        hostname: '127.0.0.1',
        port: 3000,
        path,
        method: 'POST',
        headers: Object.assign({
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'Content-Length': Buffer.byteLength(payload)
        }, headers)
      }, res => {
        let data = '';
        res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
        res.on('end', () => {
          let json = null;
          try { json = JSON.parse(data); } catch (e) { json = data; }
          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, data: json });
        });
      });
      req.on('error', reject);
      req.write(payload);
      req.end();
    });
  }

  try {
    // 1. Static files
    const indexRes = await get('/');
    console.log('GET / status:', indexRes.status, 'Has html:', 
indexRes.body.includes('BRICKFALL'));
    const cssRes = await get('/styles.css');
    console.log('GET /styles.css status:', cssRes.status, 'Has css:', 
cssRes.body.includes('--bg-main'));
    const jsRes = await get('/game.js');
    console.log('GET /game.js status:', jsRes.status, 'Has js:', 
jsRes.body.includes('GameEngine'));
    const physRes = await get('/physics.js');
    console.log('GET /physics.js status:', physRes.status, 'Has physics:', 
physRes.body.includes('circleAABBOverlap'));
    const drillRes = await get('/drills.js');
    console.log('GET /drills.js status:', drillRes.status, 'Has drills:', 
drillRes.body.includes('createDrillState'));

    // 2. Levels and Constants
    const levelsRes = await get('/api/levels');
    const lvls = JSON.parse(levelsRes.body);
    console.log('Levels count:', lvls.levels.length, 'Lvl 1 digest:', 
lvls.levels[0].digest);

    const constsRes = await get('/api/constants');
    const consts = JSON.parse(constsRes.body);
    console.log('Constants count:', consts.constants.length);

    // 3. Leaderboard
    const lbRes = await get('/api/leaderboard');
    const lb = JSON.parse(lbRes.body);
    console.log('Leaderboard top 10 count:', lb.leaderboard.length);
    console.log('Top 3 scores:', lb.leaderboard.slice(0, 3).map(x => x.initials 
+ ':' + x.score));

    // 4. Test Mira login and checkpoint
    const miraLogin = await post('/api/auth/login', { email: 
'mira@brickfall.test', password: 'password123' });
    console.log('Mira login status:', miraLogin.status, 'Revision:', 
miraLogin.data.user.revision, 'Active run level:', miraLogin.data.active_run ? 
miraLogin.data.active_run.level : null);
    console.log('Mira active run balls count:', miraLogin.data.active_run ? 
miraLogin.data.active_run.balls.length : 0);

    // 5. Test Dev login and checkpoint
    const devLogin = await post('/api/auth/login', { email: 
'dev@brickfall.test', password: 'password123' });
    console.log('Dev login status:', devLogin.status, 'Revision:', 
devLogin.data.user.revision, 'Active run level:', devLogin.data.active_run ? 
devLogin.data.active_run.level : null);

    console.log('SERVER TESTS COMPLETED SUCCESSFULLY!');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Server test error:', e);
  } finally {
    server.close();
  }
});

const http = require('http');
const crypto = require('crypto');
const app = require('/app/server.js');
const { GameEngine, getWholeSeconds, CONSTANTS } = require('/app/physics.js');
const { createDrillState, createMiraCheckpoint, createDevCheckpoint } = 
require('/app/drills.js');
const XLSX = require('xlsx');

const wb = XLSX.readFile('/assets/artifacts/brickfall_seed.xlsx');
const bricks = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets['Bricks']);

const server = app.listen(0, async () => {
  const port = server.address().port;
  console.log('Test server running on port', port);

  async function api(path, method = 'GET', body = null, token = null) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
      if (token) headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' + token;
      let payload = null;
      if (body) {
        payload = JSON.stringify(body);
        headers['Content-Length'] = Buffer.byteLength(payload);
      }

      const req = http.request({
        hostname: '127.0.0.1',
        port,
        path,
        method,
        headers
      }, res => {
        let data = '';
        res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
        res.on('end', () => {
          let json = null;
          try { json = JSON.parse(data); } catch (e) { json = data; }
          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, data: json });
        });
      });
      req.on('error', reject);
      if (payload) req.write(payload);
      req.end();
    });
  }

  let testFailures = 0;
  function assert(condition, message) {
    if (!condition) {
      console.error('FAIL:', message);
      testFailures++;
    } else {
      console.log('PASS:', message);
    }
  }

  try {
    console.log('\n=== SECTION 1: AUTHENTICATION & PLAYER ISOLATION ===');
    // Test sign-in for all 3 users
    const miraAuth = await api('/api/auth/login', 'POST', { email: 
'mira@brickfall.test', password: 'password123' });
    assert(miraAuth.status === 200, 'Mira login returns 200');
    assert(miraAuth.data.token.length === 64, 'Token is 64 characters lowercase 
hex');
    assert(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(miraAuth.data.token), 'Token matches hex 
regex');
    assert(miraAuth.data.user.initials === 'MRC', 'Mira initials are MRC');
    assert(miraAuth.data.user.highest_level === 10, 'Mira highest level is 10');
    assert(miraAuth.data.user.best_score === 24500, 'Mira best score is 24500');

    assert(devAuth.status === 200, 'Dev login returns 200');
    assert(devAuth.data.user.initials === 'DVP', 'Dev initials are DVP');
    assert(devAuth.data.user.highest_level === 3, 'Dev highest level is 3');
    assert(devAuth.data.user.best_score === 6200, 'Dev best score is 6200');

    const pollyAuth = await api('/api/auth/login', 'POST', { email: 
'polly@brickfall.test', password: 'password123' });
    assert(pollyAuth.status === 200, 'Polly login returns 200');
    assert(pollyAuth.data.user.initials === 'PLY', 'Polly initials are PLY');
    assert(pollyAuth.data.user.highest_level === 10, 'Polly highest level is 
10');
    assert(pollyAuth.data.user.best_score === 31800, 'Polly best score is 
31800');

    // Wrong password test
    const wrongAuth = await api('/api/auth/login', 'POST', { email: 
'polly@brickfall.test', password: 'wrong' });
    assert(wrongAuth.status === 401, 'Wrong password returns 401');

    // Untrusted client id test
    const meRes = await api('/api/auth/me', 'GET', null, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(meRes.data.user.id === pollyAuth.data.user.id, 'User id authenticated
solely from bearer token');

    console.log('\n=== SECTION 2: CHECKPOINTS & SEEDED FIXTURES ===');
    assert(miraAuth.data.active_run !== null, 'Mira has active paused 
checkpoint');
    assert(miraAuth.data.active_run.level === 8, 'Mira checkpoint level is 8');
    assert(miraAuth.data.active_run.score === 24500, 'Mira checkpoint score is 
24500');
    assert(miraAuth.data.active_run.lives === 1, 'Mira checkpoint lives is 1');
    assert(miraAuth.data.active_run.combo === 4, 'Mira checkpoint combo is 4');
    assert(miraAuth.data.active_run.power === 'wide', 'Mira checkpoint power is 
wide');
    assert(miraAuth.data.active_run.paddle_width === 177, 'Mira checkpoint 
paddle width is 177');

    assert(devAuth.data.active_run !== null, 'Dev has active paused 
checkpoint');
    assert(devAuth.data.active_run.level === 3, 'Dev checkpoint level is 3');
    assert(devAuth.data.active_run.score === 19950, 'Dev checkpoint score is 
19950');
    assert(devAuth.data.active_run.lives === 3, 'Dev checkpoint lives is 3');
    assert(devAuth.data.active_run.combo === 1, 'Dev checkpoint combo is 1');
    assert(devAuth.data.active_run.next_extra_life === 20000, 'Dev checkpoint 
next_extra_life is 20000');

    // Polly personal runs
    const pollyRunsRes = await api('/api/runs', 'GET', null, 
pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(pollyRunsRes.data.runs.length === 10, 'Polly personal runs stores 
exactly 10 snapshots');
    assert(pollyRunsRes.data.runs[0].run_id === 'polly-archive-11', 'Newest 
Polly run is archive-11');
    assert(pollyRunsRes.data.runs[9].run_id === 'polly-archive-02', '10th Polly 
run is archive-02 (01 pruned)');

    // Global leaderboard
    const lbRes = await api('/api/leaderboard');
    assert(lbRes.data.leaderboard.length === 10, 'Leaderboard limit is exactly 
10');
    assert(lbRes.data.leaderboard[0].initials === 'ARC' && 
lbRes.data.leaderboard[0].score === 42000, 'Top 1 is ARC 42000');
    assert(lbRes.data.leaderboard[1].initials === 'PLY' && 
lbRes.data.leaderboard[1].score === 31800, 'Top 2 is PLY 31800');
    assert(lbRes.data.leaderboard[2].initials === 'NOV' && 
lbRes.data.leaderboard[2].score === 28000, 'Top 3 is NOV 28000');
    assert(lbRes.data.leaderboard[3].initials === 'MRC' && 
lbRes.data.leaderboard[3].score === 24500, 'Top 4 is MRC 24500');
    assert(lbRes.data.leaderboard[9].initials === 'RAY' && 
lbRes.data.leaderboard[9].score === 12000, '10th is RAY 12000');
    assert(!lbRes.data.leaderboard.some(x => x.initials === 'DVP'), 'Top 10 
omits DVP 6,200');

    console.log('\n=== SECTION 3: REVISIONS, IDEMPOTENCY & TAB COORDINATION 
===');
    const startOpId = 'op-tab1-' + Date.now();

      expected_revision: pollyRev0,
      operation_id: startOpId,
      level: 1

    assert(pollyStart1.status === 200, 'Start returns 200');
    assert(pollyStart1.data.revision === pollyRev0 + 1, 'Revision increments to 
' + (pollyRev0 + 1));

    // Replay same op id & payload -> idempotent receipt
    const pollyStartReplay = await api('/api/game/start', 'POST', {
      expected_revision: pollyRev0,
      operation_id: startOpId,
      level: 1
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(pollyStartReplay.status === 200, 'Idempotent replay returns 200');
    assert(pollyStartReplay.data.revision === pollyRev0 + 1, 'Idempotent replay

    // Replay same op id with different payload -> 400
    const pollyStartDiff = await api('/api/game/start', 'POST', {
      expected_revision: pollyRev0,
      operation_id: startOpId,
      level: 2
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(pollyStartDiff.status === 400, 'Reusing operation id with different 
payload rejected with 400');

    // Stale revision -> 409 Conflict with authoritative state
    const staleMutation = await api('/api/game/start', 'POST', {
      expected_revision: pollyRev0, // old revision
      operation_id: 'op-tab2-' + Date.now(),
      level: 2
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(staleMutation.status === 409, 'Stale revision returns 409 Conflict');
    assert(staleMutation.data.current_revision === pollyRev0 + 1, '409 returns 
current_revision');
    assert(staleMutation.data.active_run !== undefined, '409 returns 
authoritative active_run');

    // Retry finish does not duplicate personal record or leaderboard row
    const finishOpId = 'op-finish-' + Date.now();
    const finishRes1 = await api('/api/game/finish', 'POST', {
      expected_revision: pollyStart1.data.revision,
      operation_id: finishOpId,
      run_id: 'polly-run-uniq-1',
      outcome: 'completed',
      level: 10,
      score: 33000,
      snapshot: { score: 33000 }
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(finishRes1.status === 200, 'Finish returns 200');

      expected_revision: finishRes1.data.revision,
      operation_id: 'op-finish-retry-' + Date.now(),
      run_id: 'polly-run-uniq-1',
      outcome: 'completed',
      level: 10,
      score: 33000,
      snapshot: { score: 33000 }
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(finishRes2.status === 200, 'Retry finish returns 200');

    const updatedLb = await api('/api/leaderboard');
    const pollyRowsWithRunId = updatedLb.data.leaderboard.filter(x => x.score 
=== 33000);
    assert(pollyRowsWithRunId.length === 1, 'Leaderboard contains exactly 1 row 
for retried finish');

    console.log('\n=== SECTION 5: MECHANICS LAB & ALL 7 DRILLS ===');
    // Drill 1

    assert(d1.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 1 advances 120 steps');
    assert(d1.score === 1400, 'Drill 1 score is 1400');
    assert(d1.combo === 1, 'Drill 1 combo reset to 1 after off-center paddle 
return');
    assert(d1.bricks.find(b => b.id === 'b5').hp === Infinity, 'Drill 1 solid 
brick remains intact');

    // Drill 2
    const d2 = new GameEngine(createDrillState('power-relay'));
    assert(d2.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 2 advance 1 is 120 steps');
    assert(d2.drops.length === 0, 'Drill 2 zero drops remaining');
    assert(d2.paddle.width === 118, 'Drill 2 paddle width restored to 118');
    assert(d2.power === 'sticky', 'Drill 2 power is sticky');
    assert(getWholeSeconds(d2.powerSeconds) === 20, 'Drill 2 sticky seconds is 
20s');
    assert(d2.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 2 advance 2 is 120 steps');
    assert(getWholeSeconds(d2.powerSeconds) === 19, 'Drill 2 sticky seconds is 
19s after 240 steps');

    // Drill 3
    const d3 = new GameEngine(createDrillState('multiball'));
    assert(d3.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 3 advance is 120 steps');
    assert(d3.balls.length === 1 && d3.balls[0].isPrimary, 'Drill 3 only primary
ball remains');
    assert(d3.lives === 3, 'Drill 3 three lives remain (no life lost)');
    assert(getWholeSeconds(d3.powerSeconds) === 9, 'Drill 3 multiball timer is 
9s');

    // Drill 4
    const d4 = new GameEngine(createDrillState('sticky-catch'));
    assert(d4.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 4 advance 1 is 120 steps');
    assert(d4.balls[0].held === true, 'Drill 4 ball is held on paddle');
    assert(getWholeSeconds(d4.powerSeconds) === 1, 'Drill 4 sticky shows 1 whole
second');
    assert(d4.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 4 advance 2 without launch is 
120 steps');
    assert(d4.power === null, 'Drill 4 sticky expired');
    assert(d4.balls[0].held === false, 'Drill 4 ball automatically released and 
in play');

    // Drill 5
    const d5 = new GameEngine(createDrillState('extra-life'));
    assert(d5.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 5 advance is 120 steps');
    assert(d5.score === 20050, 'Drill 5 score is 20050 (+100 at x1)');
    assert(d5.lives === 4, 'Drill 5 lives is 4 (+1 extra life on crossing 
20000)');
    assert(d5.combo === 2, 'Drill 5 combo is x2');
    assert(d5.nextExtraLife === 40000, 'Drill 5 threshold is 40000');

    // Drill 6
    const d6 = new GameEngine(createDrillState('last-ball'));
    const d6Steps = d6.advanceSteps(120);
    assert(d6Steps === 1, 'Drill 6 stops early after exactly 1 tick in

    assert(d6.status === 'life-lost', 'Drill 6 status is life-lost');
    assert(d6.lives === 1, 'Drill 6 lives is 1');
    assert(d6.paddle.width === 118, 'Drill 6 paddle width restored to 118');
    assert(d6.drops.length === 0, 'Drill 6 zero drops remaining');

    const d7 = new GameEngine(createDrillState('final-wall'));
    assert(d7.advanceSteps(120) <= 120, 'Drill 7 completes within 120 steps');
    assert(d7.status === 'completed', 'Drill 7 status is completed');
    assert(d7.score === 15100, 'Drill 7 score is 15100 (5000 + 100 + 10000

    server.close();

const http = require('http');
const crypto = require('crypto');
const app = require('/app/server.js');

const server = app.listen(0, async () => {
  const port = server.address().port;
  console.log('Test server running on port', port);

  async function api(path, method = 'GET', body = null, token = null) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
      if (token) headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' + token;
      let payload = null;
      if (body) {
        payload = JSON.stringify(body);
        headers['Content-Length'] = Buffer.byteLength(payload);
      }

      const req = http.request({
        hostname: '127.0.0.1',
        port,
        path,
        method,
        headers
      }, res => {
        let data = '';
        res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
        res.on('end', () => {
          let json = null;
          try { json = JSON.parse(data); } catch (e) { json = data; }
          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, data: json });
        });
      });
      req.on('error', reject);
      if (payload) req.write(payload);
      req.end();
    });
  }

  let testFailures = 0;
  function assert(condition, message) {
    if (!condition) {
      console.error('FAIL:', message);
      testFailures++;
    } else {
      console.log('PASS:', message);
    }
  }

lbRes.data.leaderboard[9].score === 12000, '10th is RAY 12000');
    assert(!lbRes.data.leaderboard.some(x => x.initials === 'DVP'), 'Top 10 
omits DVP 6,200');

    console.log('\n=== SECTION 3: REVISIONS, IDEMPOTENCY & TAB COORDINATION 
===');
    const startOpId = 'op-tab1-' + Date.now();
    const pollyRev0 = pollyAuth.data.user.revision;

    // Start level 1
    const pollyStart1 = await api('/api/game/start', 'POST', {
      expected_revision: pollyRev0,
      operation_id: startOpId,
      level: 1
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(pollyStart1.status === 200, 'Start returns 200');
    assert(pollyStart1.data.revision === pollyRev0 + 1, 'Revision increments to 
' + (pollyRev0 + 1));

    // Replay same op id & payload -> idempotent receipt
    const pollyStartReplay = await api('/api/game/start', 'POST', {
      expected_revision: pollyRev0,
      operation_id: startOpId,
      level: 1
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(pollyStartReplay.status === 200, 'Idempotent replay returns 200');
    assert(pollyStartReplay.data.revision === pollyRev0 + 1, 'Idempotent replay 
returns original revision');

    // Replay same op id with different payload -> 400
    const pollyStartDiff = await api('/api/game/start', 'POST', {
      expected_revision: pollyRev0,
      operation_id: startOpId,
      level: 2
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(pollyStartDiff.status === 400, 'Reusing operation id with different 
payload rejected with 400');

    // Stale revision -> 409 Conflict with authoritative state
    const staleMutation = await api('/api/game/start', 'POST', {
      expected_revision: pollyRev0, // old revision
      operation_id: 'op-tab2-' + Date.now(),
      level: 2
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(staleMutation.status === 409, 'Stale revision returns 409 Conflict');
    assert(staleMutation.data.current_revision === pollyRev0 + 1, '409 returns 
current_revision');
    assert(staleMutation.data.active_run !== undefined, '409 returns 
authoritative active_run');

    // Retry finish does not duplicate personal record or leaderboard row
    const finishOpId = 'op-finish-' + Date.now();
    const finishRes1 = await api('/api/game/finish', 'POST', {
      expected_revision: pollyStart1.data.revision,
      operation_id: finishOpId,
      run_id: 'polly-run-uniq-1',
      outcome: 'completed',
      level: 10,
      score: 33000,
      snapshot: { score: 33000 }
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(finishRes1.status === 200, 'Finish returns 200');

    // Retry finish with different op id but same run id
    const finishRes2 = await api('/api/game/finish', 'POST', {
      expected_revision: finishRes1.data.revision,
      operation_id: 'op-finish-retry-' + Date.now(),
      run_id: 'polly-run-uniq-1',
      outcome: 'completed',
      level: 10,
      score: 33000,
      snapshot: { score: 33000 }
    }, pollyAuth.data.token);
    assert(finishRes2.status === 200, 'Retry finish returns 200');

    const pollyRowsWithRunId = updatedLb.data.leaderboard.filter(x => x.score

=== '120', 'physics_step_hz is 120');

    console.log('\n=== SECTION 5: MECHANICS LAB & ALL 7 DRILLS ===');
    // Drill 1

    assert(d1.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 1 advances 120 steps');
    assert(d1.score === 1400, 'Drill 1 score is 1400');
    assert(d1.combo === 1, 'Drill 1 combo reset to 1 after off-center paddle

    assert(d1.bricks.find(b => b.id === 'b5').hp === Infinity, 'Drill 1 solid 
brick remains intact');

    // Drill 2
    const d2 = new GameEngine(createDrillState('power-relay'));
    assert(d2.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 2 advance 1 is 120 steps');
    assert(d2.drops.length === 0, 'Drill 2 zero drops remaining');
    assert(d2.paddle.width === 118, 'Drill 2 paddle width restored to 118');
    assert(d2.power === 'sticky', 'Drill 2 power is sticky');
    assert(getWholeSeconds(d2.powerSeconds) === 20, 'Drill 2 sticky seconds is 
20s');
    assert(d2.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 2 advance 2 is 120 steps');
    assert(getWholeSeconds(d2.powerSeconds) === 19, 'Drill 2 sticky seconds is 
19s after 240 steps');

    // Drill 3
    const d3 = new GameEngine(createDrillState('multiball'));
    assert(d3.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 3 advance is 120 steps');
    assert(d3.balls.length === 1 && d3.balls[0].isPrimary, 'Drill 3 only primary

    assert(d3.lives === 3, 'Drill 3 three lives remain (no life lost)');
    assert(getWholeSeconds(d3.powerSeconds) === 9, 'Drill 3 multiball timer is

    // Drill 4
    const d4 = new GameEngine(createDrillState('sticky-catch'));
    assert(d4.advanceSteps(120) === 120, 'Drill 4 advance 1 is 120 steps');

    assert(d4.power === null, 'Drill 4 sticky expired');
    assert(d4.balls[0].held === false, 'Drill 4 ball automatically released and

    // Drill 5

    assert(d7.advanceSteps(120) <= 120, 'Drill 7 completes within 120 steps');
    assert(d7.status === 'completed', 'Drill 7 status is completed');
    assert(d7.score === 15100, 'Drill 7 score is 15100 (5000 + 100 + 10000

    } else {

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Test server startup on port 3000

Reasoning:
Okay, here's my summary, presented as a stream of thought:

**Command Chaining Considerations**

Alright, so I'm thinking about how to string these commands together. The key 
here is efficiency and clarity, right? I've got two main options in mind: `&&` 
and `;`. Let's break down the implications of each. `&&` is the logical AND, 
meaning the subsequent command only executes if the previous one *succeeds*. 
That's great for dependency checks and ensuring a series of operations are 
carried out correctly. If one step fails, the whole process logically aborts, 
which is often desirable to avoid cascading errors. On the other hand, the 
semicolon `;` just chains them, regardless of success or failure. It executes 
each command sequentially, no matter what. So, if I just need to run things in 
order, and I don't care if a previous command fails, then the semicolon is the 
more straightforward approach. But if order of execution *and* success matter, 
I'll go with `&&`. I guess the best method depends entirely on the particular 
commands and the desired outcome. I should also keep in mind readability – if it
gets complex, I should use some kind of inline comments, or even script it out 
in a more formal manner.




$ node /app/server.js > /tmp/server.log 2>&1 & SERVER_PID=$! ; sleep 2 ; curl -s
http://localhost:3000/ | grep -q "BRICKFALL" && echo "Server responding 
successfully on port 3000!" ; kill $SERVER_PID
